, we have

\displaystyle\varphi_{l+2i}(m)

\displaystyle=-N_{l+2i}(m)\left(a_{p,1}^{(T_{1/2})}+a_{p,2}^{(T_{1/2})}\right)+(l+i-1)a_{p,2}^{(T_{1/2})}+o(1),

\displaystyle\xi_{l+2i}

\displaystyle=-a_{q,0}^{(T_{1/2})}+O\left(\frac{1}{\ln k}\right),

(315)

and thus by (
66b
), (
66d
) in Induction Hypothesis I and our choice of
T_{1/2}
, for all
i\in[k-l]
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{P}}\Big(a_{l+2i}\notin\{a_{1},a_{2},\cdots,a_{l-1},\texttt{up}\}\Big|\bigcap_{j=1}^{2i}{\mathcal{B}}_{j}\Big)

\displaystyle=1-(1+o(1))\frac{\exp\left(-a_{q,0}^{(T_{1/2})}-\frac{l+i-1}{l+2i}a_{p,2}^{(T_{1/2})}\right)+(l-1)\exp\left(-\frac{a_{p,1}^{(T_{1/2})}+a_{p,2}^{(T_{1/2})}}{l+2i}\right)}{\exp\left(-a_{q,0}^{(T_{1/2})}-\frac{l+i-1}{l+2i}a_{p,2}^{(T_{1/2})}\right)+(l+i-1)\exp\left(-\frac{a_{p,1}^{(T_{1/2})}+a_{p,2}^{(T_{1/2})}}{l+2i}\right)+k-l-i+1}

\displaystyle\geq 1-\frac{(1+o(1))l}{k^{2}},

(316)

and

\displaystyle{\mathbb{P}}\Big(a_{2k-l+2}\in{\mathcal{A}}_{2k-l+2,\times}\Big|\bigcap_{j=1}^{2(k-l+1)}{\mathcal{B}}_{j}\Big)

\displaystyle=1-(1+o(1))\frac{(l-1)\exp\left(-\frac{a_{p,1}^{(T_{1/2})}}{l+2(k-l+1)}\right)+\exp\left(-a_{q,0}^{(T_{1/2})}-\frac{k}{l+2(k-l+1)}a_{p,2}^{(T_{1/2})}\right)}{k\exp\left(-\frac{a_{p,1}^{(T_{1/2})}}{l+2(k-l+1)}\right)+\exp\left(-a_{q,0}^{(T_{1/2})}-\frac{k}{l+2(k-l+1)}a_{p,2}^{(T_{1/2})}\right)}

\displaystyle=1-(1+o(1))\frac{(l-1)\exp\left(-\frac{a_{p,1}^{(T_{1/2})}}{2k}\right)+\exp\left(-a_{q,0}^{(T_{1/2})}-\frac{1}{2}a_{p,2}^{(T_{1/2})}\right)}{k\exp\left(-\frac{a_{p,1}^{(T_{1/2})}}{2k}\right)+\exp\left(-a_{q,0}^{(T_{1/2})}-\frac{1}{2}a_{p,2}^{(T_{1/2})}\right)}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:a_p_2_bound_T_12}}{=}1-(1+o(1))\frac{l-1+\exp\left(-a_{q,0}^{(T_{1/2})}\right)}{k+\exp\left(-a_{q,0}^{(T_{1/2})}\right)}

\displaystyle=1-\frac{(1+o(1))l}{k}.

(317)

Plugging (
A.3.2
), (
A.3.2
) and (
A.3.2
) into (
A.3.2
), we deduce

\displaystyle 1-R\left(\pi^{(T_{1/2})};{\mathcal{P}}_{l}\right)\geq 1-\frac{c_{2}l}{k}

(318)

for some constant

. This gives (

).

A.3.3
Proof of Lemma
5

Gradient computation.

Note that when
l=1
, for all even steps, the agent is at a leaf node. If it’s not a goal node, then the agent needs to go up, or the environment terminates. Thus for any
h\in[2k]
, conditioned on
{\mathcal{E}}_{1,h}
,
N_{h,\times}=\lfloor h/2\rfloor
. Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\delta a_{q,0}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:delta_a_q_0_simplified_balanced}}{=}\-{\mathbb{E}}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbbm{1}\{c_{h}=\times\}\frac{e^{-a_{c,1}^{(t)}}}{e^{a_{c,1}^{(t)}}+e^{-a_{c,1}^{(t)}}}\pi_{h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})(1-\pi_{h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up}))Q_{h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\bigg|l=1\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+{\mathbb{E}}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbbm{1}\{c_{h}=0\}\frac{(h+1)e^{a_{c,0}^{(t)}}}{(h-1)e^{-a_{c,0}^{(t)}}+(h+1)e^{a_{c,0}^{(t)}}}\pi_{h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})G_{h}^{(t)}\bigg|l=1\right].

(319)

With the notation we define at the beginning of Appendix
A.3.2
, by the above equation, we have

\displaystyle\delta a_{q,0}^{(t)}

\displaystyle=\pi_{1,1}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})V_{1,1}^{(t)}+\sum_{h=1}^{k}\bigg({\mathbb{P}}^{(t)}\left({\mathcal{E}}_{1,2h+1}\right)\frac{1}{1+\frac{h}{h+1}e^{-2a_{c,0}^{(t)}}}\pi_{1,2h+1}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})

\displaystyle\qquad-{\mathbb{P}}^{(t)}\left({\mathcal{E}}_{1,2h}\right)\frac{1}{1+e^{2a_{c,1}^{(t)}}}\pi_{1,2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\left(1-\pi_{1,2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\right)\bigg)V_{1,2h+1}^{(t)}

\displaystyle=\pi_{1,1}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})V_{1,1}^{(t)}+\sum_{h=1}^{k-1}{\mathbb{P}}^{(t)}\left({\mathcal{E}}_{1,2h}\right)\pi_{1,2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\bigg(\frac{1}{1+\frac{h}{h+1}e^{-2a_{c,0}^{(t)}}}\pi_{1,2h+1}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})-\frac{1}{1+e^{2a_{c,1}^{(t)}}}\left(1-\pi_{1,2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\right)\bigg)V_{1,2h+1}^{(t)},

(320)

where the second relation uses

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}^{\pi^{(t)}}[Q_{2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})|{\mathcal{E}}_{1,2h}]=V_{1,2h+1}^{(t)}

(321)

by (
130
), and the third relation follows from the fact that

\displaystyle{\mathbb{P}}^{(t)}\left({\mathcal{E}}_{1,2h+1}\right)=\pi_{1,2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up}){\mathbb{P}}^{(t)}\left({\mathcal{E}}_{1,2h}\right).

(322)

This gives (
131a
).

Similarly, we have

\displaystyle\delta a_{q,\times}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:delta_a_q_times_simplified_balanced}}{=}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbbm{1}\{c_{h}=\times\}\frac{e^{a_{c,1}^{(t)}}}{e^{a_{c,1}^{(t)}}+e^{-a_{c,1}^{(t)}}}\pi_{h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})(1-\pi_{h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up}))Q_{h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\bigg|l=1\right]

\displaystyle\qquad-{\mathbb{E}}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbbm{1}\{c_{h}=0\}\frac{(h-1)e^{-a_{c,0}^{(t)}}}{(h-1)e^{-a_{c,0}^{(t)}}+(h+1)e^{a_{c,0}^{(t)}}}\pi_{h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})G_{h}^{(t)}\bigg|l=1\right],

(323)

which gives (
131b
).

\displaystyle\delta a_{c,0}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:delta_a_c_0_simplified_balanced}}{=}\left(1+\frac{1}{k}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbbm{1}\{c_{h}=0\}\frac{(h-1)(h+1)}{\left((h-1)e^{-a_{c,0}^{(t)}}+(h+1)e^{a_{c,0}^{(t)}}\right)^{2}}\left(a_{q,\times}^{(t)}+a_{q,0}^{(t)}\right)\pi_{h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})G_{h}^{(t)}\bigg|l=1\right],

(324)

which gives (
131c
).

\displaystyle\delta a_{c,1}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:delta_a_c_1_simplified_balanced}}{=}\left(1+\frac{1}{k}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbbm{1}\{c_{h}=\times\}\frac{1}{\left(e^{a_{c,1}^{(t)}}+e^{-a_{c,1}^{(t)}}\right)^{2}}\left(a_{q,\times}^{(t)}+a_{q,0}^{(t)}\right)\pi_{h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})(1-\pi_{h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up}))Q_{h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\bigg|l=1\right],

(325)

which gives (
131d
).

\displaystyle\delta a_{p,0}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:delta_a_p_0_simplified_balanced}}{=}(1+o(1)){\mathbb{E}}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\frac{\pi_{h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})}{h}\left(Q_{h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})-G_{h}^{(t)}\right)\bigg|l=1\right],

(326)

which gives (
131e
).

\displaystyle\delta a_{p,1}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:delta_a_p_1_simplified_balanced}}{=}(1+o(1))\Bigg({\mathbb{E}}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbbm{1}\{c_{h}=0\}\frac{1}{k}\frac{\lfloor h/2\rfloor}{h}\pi_{h}^{(t)}(j)G_{h}^{(t)}\bigg|j\in{\mathcal{A}}_{h,\times},l=1\right]

\displaystyle\quad\quad+{\mathbb{E}}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbbm{1}\{c_{h}=\times\}\frac{N_{h}(j)}{h}\pi_{h}^{(t)}(j)\pi_{h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})Q_{h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\bigg|j\in[k],l=1\right]\Bigg)

\displaystyle=(1+o(1))\Bigg({\mathbb{E}}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{k-1}\frac{1}{k}\frac{h}{2h+1}\pi_{2h+1}^{(t)}(j)G_{2h+1}^{(t)}\bigg|j\in{\mathcal{A}}_{2h+1,\times},l=1\right]

\displaystyle\quad\quad+{\mathbb{E}}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{k-1}\frac{1}{2k}\pi_{2h}^{(t)}(j)\pi_{2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})Q_{2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\bigg|j\in{\mathcal{A}}_{2h-1,\times}\cup\{a_{2h-1}\},l=1\right]\Bigg),

(327)

where for the even-step term we use

\{N_{2h}(j)=1\}=\{j\in{\mathcal{A}}_{2h-1,\times}\cup\{a_{2h-1}\}\},\qquad{\mathbb{P}}\left(N_{2h}(j)=1\right)=\frac{h}{k}.

On this event,

\pi_{2h}^{(t)}(j)=\pi_{1,2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{down}_{\times}^{1}).

For the odd-step term, we use

{\mathbb{P}}\left(j\in{\mathcal{A}}_{2h+1,\times}\right)=\frac{h}{k},\qquad\pi_{2h+1}^{(t)}(j)=\pi_{1,2h+1}^{(t)}(\texttt{down}_{\times}).

Therefore, (
A.3.3
) gives (
131f
). Similarly,

\displaystyle\delta a_{p,3}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:delta_a_p_3_simplified_balanced}}{=}(1+o(1))\Bigg({\mathbb{E}}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbbm{1}\{c_{h}=0\}\frac{1}{k}\frac{\lfloor h/2\rfloor}{h}\pi_{h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\left(Q_{h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})-G_{h}^{(t)}\right)\bigg|j\in{\mathcal{A}}_{h,\times},l=1\right]

\displaystyle\quad\quad+{\mathbb{E}}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbbm{1}\{c_{h}=\times\}\frac{1}{k}\frac{\lfloor h/2\rfloor}{h}\pi_{h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\left(Q_{h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})-G_{h}^{(t)}\right)\bigg|N_{h}(j)=1,l=1\right]\Bigg)

\displaystyle=(1+o(1))\Bigg(-{\mathbb{E}}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{k-1}\frac{1}{k}\frac{h}{2h+1}\pi_{2h+1}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})G_{2h+1}^{(t)}\bigg|j\in{\mathcal{A}}_{2h+1,\times},l=1\right]

\displaystyle\quad\quad+{\mathbb{E}}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{k-1}\frac{1}{2k}\pi_{2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\left(1-\pi_{2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\right)Q_{2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\bigg|j\in{\mathcal{A}}_{2h-1,\times}\cup\{a_{2h-1}\},l=1\right]\Bigg),

(328)

which gives (
131g
).

\displaystyle\delta a_{p,\texttt{up}}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:delta_a_p_up_simplified_balanced}}{=}-(1+o(1)){\mathbb{E}}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\pi_{h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\frac{\lfloor(h-1)/2\rfloor}{h}\left(Q_{h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})-G_{h}^{(t)}\right)\bigg|l=1\right],

(329)

which gives (
131h
).

To compute the simplified form of
\delta a_{b,i}^{(t)}
for
i\in\{0,1,2\}
, we first simplify
\beta_{h,i}^{(t)}
for
i\in\{0,1,2\}
defined in (

), (

) and (

) for

as follows for all

:

\displaystyle\beta_{2h-1,0}^{(t)}

\displaystyle=-\frac{a_{p,0}^{(t)}}{k}\pi_{2h-1}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})G_{2h-1}^{(t)},

(330a)

\displaystyle\quad\beta_{2h,0}^{(t)}

\displaystyle=\frac{a_{p,0}^{(t)}}{k}\pi_{2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\left(1-\pi_{2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\right)Q_{2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up}),

(330b)

\displaystyle\beta_{2h-1,1}^{(t)}

\displaystyle=\left(\left(a_{p,1}^{(t)}+a_{p,2}^{(t)}\right)\pi_{2h-1}^{(t)}(\texttt{down}_{\times})+a_{p,2}^{(t)}\pi_{2h-1}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\right)(h-1)G_{2h-1}^{(t)},

(330c)

\displaystyle\beta_{2h,1}^{(t)}

\displaystyle=0,

(330d)

\displaystyle\beta_{2h-1,2}^{(t)}

\displaystyle=\frac{h-1}{k}a_{p,\texttt{up}}^{(t)}\pi_{2h-1}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})G_{2h-1}^{(t)},

(330e)

\displaystyle\beta_{2h,2}^{(t)}

\displaystyle=\left(-\left(ha_{p,2}^{(t)}+\frac{h-1}{k}a_{p,\texttt{up}}^{(t)}\right)\left(1-\pi_{2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\right)+\left(a_{p,1}^{(t)}+a_{p,2}^{(t)}\right)h\pi_{2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{down}_{\times}^{1})\right)\pi_{2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})Q_{2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up}).

(330f)

By the above expressions and

\displaystyle\delta a_{b,0}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:delta_a_b_0_simplified_balanced}}{=}\frac{1+o(1)}{N}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\frac{1}{h^{2}}\left((h-1)\beta_{h,0}^{(t)}-\beta_{h,1}^{(t)}-\beta_{h,2}^{(t)}\right)\right],

\displaystyle\delta a_{b,1}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:delta_a_b_1_simplified_balanced}}{=}\frac{1+o(1)}{N}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{k-1}\frac{1}{(2h+1)^{2}}\left((h+1)\beta_{2h+1,1}^{(t)}-h\beta_{2h+1,0}^{(t)}-h\beta_{2h+1,2}^{(t)}\right)\right],

we obtain (

) and (

).

Policy computation.

By Induction Hypothesis I, when
l=1
, (
1
) can be simplified to (recall we assume

)

\displaystyle\forall i\in[k]:\quad\varphi_{2h-1}^{(t)}(i)

\displaystyle=-\frac{a_{p,0}^{(t)}}{k}+(h-1)\frac{a_{p,\texttt{up}}^{(t)}}{k}+(h-1)a_{p,2}^{(t)}-N_{2h-1}(i)\left(a_{p,1}^{(t)}+a_{p,2}^{(t)}\right)+O\left(\frac{\ln^{3}k}{k}\right),

(331)

\displaystyle\varphi_{2h}^{(t)}(i)

\displaystyle=-\frac{a_{p,0}^{(t)}}{k}+(h-1)\frac{a_{p,\texttt{up}}^{(t)}}{k}+ha_{p,2}^{(t)}-N_{2h}(i)\left(a_{p,1}^{(t)}+a_{p,2}^{(t)}\right)+O\left(\frac{\ln^{3}k}{k}\right),

(332)

and (

) can be simplified to (

).
By Induction Hypothesis I we know that

\displaystyle\forall h\in[k]:\quad\left|\frac{1}{k}\xi_{h}^{(t)}\right|\lesssim\frac{\ln k}{k},\quad\left|\frac{a_{p,0}^{(t)}}{k}\right|\lesssim\frac{\ln k}{k},\quad\left|\frac{a_{p,\texttt{up}}^{(t)}}{k}\right|\lesssim\frac{\ln k}{k}.

(333)

), (

) with (

)-(

) in Lemma

, we have (

), (

A.3.4
Proof of Lemma
6

We drop the superscript
(t)
for simplicity.
By the definition of
\widehat{G}_{h}
in (
A.2
) and the definition of
V_{1,h}
in (
130
), we have the following recursion:

\displaystyle V_{1,2k+1}=0,\quad\forall h\leq k:\,\,V_{1,2h-1}=\underbrace{\pi_{1,2h-1}(\texttt{down})}_{(i)}+\underbrace{(k-h)\pi_{1,2h-1}(\texttt{down})}_{(ii)}\pi_{1,2h}(\texttt{up})V_{1,2h+1}.

(334)

where
(i)
is the probability of the goal is reached after taking some down actions
i\in[k]\setminus{\mathcal{A}}_{h,\times}
, and
(ii)
is the probability of the goal is not reached after taking a down action
i\in[k]\setminus{\mathcal{A}}_{h,\times}
(note if
up
or
i\in{\mathcal{A}}_{h,\times}
is taken, the goal will not be reached and the environment terminates).

To simplify notation, we define

\displaystyle y_{h}\coloneqq V_{1,2h-1},\quad r_{h}=1+(k-h)\pi_{1,2h}(\texttt{up})V_{1,2h+1},\quad\beta_{h}=(k-h)\pi_{1,2h}(\texttt{up})\pi_{1,2h+1}(\texttt{down}),

then

\displaystyle\frac{y_{h}}{y_{1}}=\frac{\pi_{1,2h-1}(\texttt{down})r_{h}}{\pi_{1,1}(\texttt{down})r_{1}},

(335)

and

\displaystyle r_{k}=1,\quad r_{h}=1+\beta_{h}r_{h+1}.

If
\pi_{1,2}\lesssim\frac{1}{\ln k}
, then by (
141
),

\displaystyle\forall h\in[k]:\quad\pi_{1,2h}(\texttt{up})\lesssim\frac{1}{\ln k}.

This suggests
\beta_{h}\lesssim\frac{1}{\ln k}
for any
h\in[k-1]
.
Thus we have

\displaystyle r_{1}=1+\beta_{1}r_{2}=\cdots=1+\beta_{1}+\beta_{1}\beta_{2}+\cdots+\beta_{1}\beta_{2}\cdots\beta_{k-1}=1+O\left(\frac{1}{\ln k}\right).

Similarly, we have
r_{h}=1+O(\frac{1}{\ln k})
for any
h\in[k]
. Thus
r_{h}/r_{1}=1+O(\frac{1}{\ln k})
.
Moreover, when
s-1\leq T_{1,1}
, by Induction Hypothesis I we have

\displaystyle\pi_{1,2h-1}(\texttt{down})=(1+o(1))\frac{1}{k},\quad\pi_{1,1}(\texttt{down})=(1+o(1))\frac{1}{k}.

Thus
\pi_{1,2h-1}(\texttt{down})/\pi_{1,1}(\texttt{down})=1+o(1)
.
Therefore, by (
335
),
y_{h}/y_{1}=1+o(1)
. This gives (
155
).

If
\pi_{1,2}^{(s-1)}\gtrsim\frac{1}{\ln k}
, then since
s-1\leq T_{1,1}
, by (
141
), we have

\displaystyle\forall h\in[k]:\quad\pi_{1,2h}(\texttt{up})\leq\frac{1+o(1)}{2},

which indicates
r_{1}\asymp 1
,
r_{h}\asymp 1
for any
h\in[k]
. Thus by (
335
), (
154
) holds.

A.3.5
Proof of Lemma
3

We show Lemma
3
by induction. First by Lemma
2
we know that Lemma
3
holds for
t=T_{1/2}
. We make the following induction hypothesis:

Induction Hypothesis II:
For all
t\leq s-1
(
s\in[T_{1/2}+1,T_{1}]
), the relations in Lemma
3
hold.

Below we show Lemma
3
holds for
t=s
under Induction Hypothesis II. We still follow the notation defined at the beginning of Appendix
A.3.2
. We first simplify the gradients for Phase 1.2.

Gradients/policy simplification for phase 1.2.

Similar as Lemma
5
, by Induction Hypothesis II, using the notation we define at the beginning of Appendix
A.3.2
, we could further simplify the gradients and policy expressions in Lemma
1
when
l=1
. We summarize the results in the following lemma.

Lemma 7
.

Under Induction Hypothesis II, we have for any
t\in[T_{1/2},s-1]
:

\displaystyle\delta a_{q,0}^{(t)}

\displaystyle=\left(1+o\left(1\right)\right)\Bigg(\pi_{1,1}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})+\sum_{h=1}^{k-1}\frac{k-h}{k}\bigg(\frac{1}{1+\frac{h}{h+1}e^{-2a_{c,0}^{(t)}}}\pi_{1,2h+1}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})-\frac{1}{1+e^{2a_{c,1}^{(t)}}}\left(1-\pi_{1,2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\right)\bigg)\Bigg),

(336a)

\displaystyle\delta a_{q,\times}^{(t)}

\displaystyle=\left(1+o\left(1\right)\right)\sum_{h=1}^{k-1}\frac{k-h}{k}\bigg(\frac{1}{1+e^{-2a_{c,1}^{(t)}}}\left(1-\pi_{1,2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\right)-\frac{1}{1+\frac{h+1}{h}e^{2a_{c,0}^{(t)}}}\pi_{1,2h+1}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\bigg),

(336b)

\displaystyle\delta a_{c,0}^{(t)}

\displaystyle=\left(1+o\left(1\right)\right)\sum_{h=1}^{k-1}\frac{k-h}{k}\frac{h(h+1)}{\left(he^{-a_{c,0}^{(t)}}+(h+1)e^{a_{c,0}^{(t)}}\right)^{2}}\left(a_{q,\times}^{(t)}+a_{q,0}^{(t)}\right)\pi_{1,2h+1}^{(t)}(\texttt{up}),

(336c)

\displaystyle\delta a_{c,1}^{(t)}

\displaystyle=\left(1+o\left(1\right)\right)\sum_{h=1}^{k-1}\frac{k-h}{k}\frac{1}{\left(e^{a_{c,1}^{(t)}}+e^{-a_{c,1}^{(t)}}\right)^{2}}\left(a_{q,\times}^{(t)}+a_{q,0}^{(t)}\right)(1-\pi_{1,2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})),

(336d)

\displaystyle\delta a_{p,0}^{(t)}

\displaystyle=\left(1+o\left(1\right)\right)\Bigg(-\pi_{1,1}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})+\sum_{h=1}^{k-1}\frac{k-h}{k}\Bigg(\frac{e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}}{e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}+(2h-1)e^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}\left(1-\pi_{1,2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 179.25244pt-\frac{e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}}{e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}+he^{a_{b,1}^{(t)}}+he^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}\pi_{1,2h+1}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\Bigg)\Bigg),

(336e)

\displaystyle\delta a_{p,1}^{(t)}

\displaystyle=\frac{1+o(1)}{k}\sum_{h=1}^{k-1}\frac{k-h}{k}\Bigg(\frac{he^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}{e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}+(2h-1)e^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}\pi_{1,2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{down}_{\times}^{1})+\frac{he^{a_{b,1}^{(t)}}}{e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}+he^{a_{b,1}^{(t)}}+he^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}\pi_{1,2h+1}^{(t)}(\texttt{down}_{\times})\Bigg),

(336f)

\displaystyle\delta a_{p,3}^{(t)}

\displaystyle=\frac{1+o(1)}{k}\sum_{h=1}^{k-1}\frac{k-h}{k}\Bigg(\frac{he^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}{e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}+(2h-1)e^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}\left(1-\pi_{1,2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\right)-\frac{he^{a_{b,1}^{(t)}}}{e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}+he^{a_{b,1}^{(t)}}+he^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}\pi_{1,2h+1}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\Bigg),

(336g)

\displaystyle\delta a_{p,\texttt{up}}^{(t)}

\displaystyle=\left(1+o(1)\right)\sum_{h=1}^{k-1}\frac{k-h}{k}\Bigg(\frac{he^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}{e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}+he^{a_{b,1}^{(t)}}+he^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}\pi_{1,2h+1}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})-\frac{(h-1)e^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}{e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}+(2h-1)e^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}\left(1-\pi_{1,2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\right)\Bigg),

(336h)

\displaystyle\delta a_{b,0}^{(t)}

\displaystyle=\frac{1+o(1)}{N}\sum_{h=1}^{k-1}\frac{k-h}{k}I_{h,0}^{(t)},

(336i)

\displaystyle\delta a_{b,1}^{(t)}

\displaystyle=\frac{1+o(1)}{N}\sum_{h=1}^{k-1}\frac{k-h}{k}I_{h,1}^{(t)},

(336j)

where

\displaystyle I_{h,0}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\frac{e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}e^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}{\left(e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}+(2h-1)e^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}\right)^{2}}\left(\left(\frac{2h-1}{k}a_{p,0}^{(t)}+ha_{p,2}^{(t)}+\frac{h-1}{k}a_{p,\texttt{up}}^{(t)}\right)\left(1-\pi_{1,2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\right)-\left(a_{p,1}^{(t)}+a_{p,2}^{(t)}\right)h\pi_{1,2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{down}_{\times}^{1})\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt-\frac{he^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}}{\left(e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}+he^{a_{b,1}^{(t)}}+he^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}\right)^{2}}\Bigg[\left(e^{a_{b,1}^{(t)}}\left(a_{p,2}^{(t)}+\frac{a_{p,0}^{(t)}}{k}\right)+e^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}\left(\frac{a_{p,\texttt{up}}^{(t)}}{k}+\frac{a_{p,0}^{(t)}}{k}\right)\right)\pi_{1,2h+1}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})

\displaystyle\hskip 179.25244pt+e^{a_{b,1}^{(t)}}\left(a_{p,1}^{(t)}+a_{p,2}^{(t)}\right)\pi_{1,2h+1}^{(t)}(\texttt{down}_{\times})\Bigg],

(337)

\displaystyle I_{h,1}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\frac{he^{a_{b,1}^{(t)}}}{\left(e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}+he^{a_{b,1}^{(t)}}+he^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}\right)^{2}}\Bigg[\left(e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}+he^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}\right)\left(a_{p,1}^{(t)}+a_{p,2}^{(t)}\right)\pi_{1,2h+1}^{(t)}(\texttt{down}_{\times})

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt\hskip 18.49988pt+\Bigg(e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}\Bigg(a_{p,2}^{(t)}+\frac{a_{p,0}^{(t)}}{k}\Bigg)+he^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}\Bigg(a_{p,2}^{(t)}-\frac{a_{p,\texttt{up}}^{(t)}}{k}\Bigg)\Bigg)\pi_{1,2h+1}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\Bigg].

(338)

For all
\forall h\in[k]
,
i\in[k]
, we have

\displaystyle\pi_{1,h}^{(t)}\coloneqq\mathsf{softmax}\left(\overline{\phi}_{h}^{(t)}\right),

(339)

is defined as

\displaystyle\forall i\in[k]:\quad\overline{\phi}_{2h-1,i}^{(t)}\coloneqq-\frac{N_{2h-1}(i)(a_{p,1}^{(t)}+a_{p,2}^{(t)})e^{a_{b,1}^{(t)}}}{e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}+(h-1)e^{a_{b,1}^{(t)}}+(h-1)e^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}},\quad\overline{\phi}_{2h,i}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\coloneqq-\frac{N_{2h}(i)(a_{p,1}^{(t)}+a_{p,2}^{(t)})e^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}{e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}+(2h-1)e^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}.

(340)

\displaystyle\overline{\phi}_{h,\texttt{up}}^{(t)}

(341)

\displaystyle\pi_{1,1}^{(s-1)}(\texttt{up})

(354)

\displaystyle\forall h\in[k-1]:\quad\frac{k-h}{k}\pi_{1,2h+1}^{(s-1)}(\texttt{up})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{k-h}{k}\frac{\exp\left(-\left(1+O\left(\frac{1}{\ln^{2}k}\right)\right)a_{q,0}^{(s-1)}-\frac{h}{2h+1}a_{p,2}^{(s-1)}\right)}{k-h}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\exp\left(-\left(1+\frac{1}{k}\right)a_{q,0}^{(s-1)}-\left(\frac{1}{3}+O\left(\frac{1}{\ln^{2}k}\right)\right)a_{p,2}^{(s-1)}\right)}{k}

(355)

Similarly, from Lemma

Analogously, we have

t\in[T_{1,3}^{\prime},T_{1,3}]
. We first show there exists
t_{0}\in[T_{1/2}+1,T_{1,3}^{\prime}]
such that

\displaystyle\left(1+\frac{1}{k}\right)a_{q,0}^{(t)}-\frac{a_{p,0}^{(t)}}{k}+0.05\sqrt{a_{p,1}^{(t)}/k}+3\ln k\geq\min\left\{a_{q,\times}^{(t)}-\mu_{2}^{(t)},a_{q,\times}^{(t)}-\mu_{2(k-1)}^{(t)}\right\}

(375)

holds at step
t_{0}
. Suppose otherwise, i.e., for all
t\in[T_{1/2},T_{1,3}^{\prime}]
, we have

\displaystyle\left(1+\frac{1}{k}\right)a_{q,0}^{(t)}-\frac{a_{p,0}^{(t)}}{k}+0.05\sqrt{a_{p,1}^{(t)}/k}+3\ln k<\min\left\{a_{q,\times}^{(t)}-\mu_{2}^{(t)},a_{q,\times}^{(t)}-\mu_{2(k-1)}^{(t)}\right\}

(376)

Then for all
t\in[T_{1/2},T_{1,3}^{\prime}]
:

\displaystyle\pi_{1,1}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:pi_1_1_up_phase1.2}}{=}(1+o(1))\frac{1}{k}\exp\left(-\left(1+\frac{1}{k}\right)a_{q,0}^{(t)}+\frac{a_{p,0}^{(t)}}{k}\right)

\displaystyle\geq(1+o(1))k^{2}\exp\left(-\min\left\{a_{q,\times}^{(t)}-\mu_{2}^{(t)},a_{q,\times}^{(t)}-\mu_{2(k-1)}^{(t)}\right\}+0.05\sqrt{a_{p,1}^{(t)}/k}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:sum_1-pi_2h_up_ub}}{\geq}2(1+o(1))\exp\left(0.05\sqrt{a_{p,1}^{(t)}/k}\right)\sum_{h=1}^{k-1}\frac{k-h}{k}\left(1-\pi_{1,2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\right).

(377)

Thus by (
A.3.5
), (
336a
) and (
336b
), we have

\displaystyle\forall t\in[T_{1/2},T_{1,3}^{\prime}]:\quad\delta a_{q,0}^{(t)}\geq 2(1+o(1))\exp\left(0.05\sqrt{a_{p,1}^{(t)}/k}\right)\delta a_{q,\times}^{(t)}.

(378)

Note that from (
66a
) and (
66f
) we know that

\displaystyle a_{q,\times}^{(T_{1/2})}\leq 13.3\ln k,

(379)

and from (
73a
) and our choice of
T_{1,3},T_{1,3}^{\prime}
we know that

\displaystyle a_{q,\times}^{(T_{1,3})}\geq 14.4\ln k,\quad a_{q,\times}^{(T_{1,3}^{\prime})}\geq 13.95\ln k.

(380)

By (
379
), (
380
) and (
378
), we have

\displaystyle a_{q,0}^{(T_{1,3}^{\prime})}>a_{q,0}^{(T_{1,3}^{\prime})}-a_{q,0}^{(T_{1/2})}

\displaystyle\gtrsim\exp\left(0.05\sqrt{a_{p,1}^{(T_{1,3}^{\prime})}/k}\right)\left(a_{q,\times}^{(T_{1,3}^{\prime})}-a_{q,\times}^{(T_{1/2})}\right)

\displaystyle\gtrsim\exp\left(0.05\sqrt{a_{p,1}^{(T_{1,3}^{\prime})}/k}\right)\ln k\gg\ln k.

(381)

But by (
72b
), our choice of
T_{1,3}
(c.f. (
69a
)) and (
72h
), we have

\displaystyle a_{q,0}^{(T_{1,3}^{\prime})}\asymp a_{p,2}^{(T_{1,3}^{\prime})}\asymp\ln k.

(382)

The above two relations contradict each other, and hence there must exist
t_{0}\in[T_{1/2}+1,T_{1,3}^{\prime}]
such that (
375
) holds at step
t_{0}
. We can choose
t_{0}\in[T_{1/2}+1,T_{1,3}^{\prime}]
to be the biggest step such that (
375
) holds, and show that (
74e
) holds for all
t\in[t_{0},T_{1,3}]
. Suppose otherwise, i.e., there exists
t_{1}\in[t_{0}+1,T_{1,3}^{\prime}]
such that (
74e
) does not hold at step
t_{1}
, then by Induction Hypothesis II we have

\displaystyle\left(1+\frac{1}{k}\right)a_{q,0}^{(t_{1})}+0.1\sqrt{a_{p,1}^{(t_{1})}/k}+3\ln k+o(1)

\displaystyle=\left(1+\frac{1}{k}\right)a_{q,0}^{(t_{1})}-\frac{a_{p,0}^{(t_{1})}}{k}+0.1\sqrt{a_{p,1}^{(t_{1})}/k}+3\ln k

\displaystyle<\min\left\{a_{q,\times}^{(t_{1})}-\mu_{2}^{(t_{1})},a_{q,\times}^{(t_{1})}-\mu_{2(k-1)}^{(t_{1})}\right\}

\displaystyle=a_{q,\times}^{(t_{1})}-\frac{a_{p,1}^{(t_{1})}}{2k}+o(1).

(383)

By our choice of
t_{0}
, gradient update rule and Induction Hypothesis II, we have for all
t\in[t_{0},t_{1}-1]
:

\displaystyle\left(1+\frac{1}{k}\right)a_{q,0}^{(t)}+0.05\sqrt{a_{p,1}^{(t)}/k}+3\ln k+o(1)

\displaystyle\leq a_{q,\times}^{(t)}-\frac{a_{p,1}^{(t)}}{2k}.

(384)

Then similar as how we obtain (
378
), from the above relation and (
336a
), we can compute that

\displaystyle\forall t\in[t_{0},t_{1}-1]:\quad\delta a_{q,0}^{(t)}\gtrsim\exp\left(0.05\sqrt{a_{p,1}^{(t)}/k}\right)\delta a_{q,\times}^{(t)}.

(385)

and thus we have

\displaystyle\left(1+\frac{1}{k}\right)a_{q,0}^{(t_{1})}+0.05\sqrt{a_{p,1}^{(t_{1})}/k}+3\ln k-\left(a_{q,\times}^{(t_{1})}-\frac{a_{p,1}^{(t_{1})}}{2k}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:a_q_0>a_q_x_bound_T_13_tight}}{\geq}o(1)+\eta\sum_{t=t_{0}}^{t_{1}-1}\left(\delta a_{q,0}^{(t)}-\delta a_{q,\times}^{(t)}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:delta_a_q_0_t_1-1_contradiction_T_13}}{\gtrsim}\eta\exp\left(0.05\sqrt{a_{p,1}^{(t_{0})}/k}\right)\sum_{t=t_{0}}^{t_{1}-1}\delta a_{q,\times}^{(t)}+o(1)>o(1),

(386)

which implies

\displaystyle\left(1+\frac{1}{k}\right)a_{q,0}^{(t_{1})}+0.1\sqrt{a_{p,1}^{(t_{1})}/k}+3\ln k\geq a_{q,\times}^{(t_{1})}-\frac{a_{p,1}^{(t_{1})}}{2k}+0.05\sqrt{a_{p,1}^{(t_{1})}/k}+o(1).

(387)

This contradicts (
A.3.5
). Therefore, we have shown that (
74e
) holds for all
t\in[T_{1,3}^{\prime},T_{1,3}]
.

Then for all
t\in[T_{1,3}^{\prime},s-1]
:

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{k-1}\frac{k-h}{k}\left(1-\pi_{1,2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\right)

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:sum_1-pi_2h_up_lb}}{\geq}\left(1+o(1)\right)\exp\left(-\min\left\{a_{q,\times}^{(t)}-\mu_{2}^{(t)}-\ln k,a_{q,\times}^{(t)}-\mu_{2(k-1)}^{(t)}+\ln k\right\}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1+o(1)}{k}\exp\left(-\min\left\{a_{q,\times}^{(t)}-\mu_{2}^{(t)},a_{q,\times}^{(t)}-\mu_{2(k-1)}^{(t)}\right\}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:a_q_0>a_q_x_bound_T_13}}{\geq}\frac{1+o(1)}{k^{4}}\exp\left(-\left(1+\frac{1}{k}\right)a_{q,0}^{(t)}+\frac{a_{p,0}^{(t)}}{k}-0.1\sqrt{a_{p,1}^{(t)}/k}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:pi_1_1_up_phase1.2}}{=}\frac{1+o(1)}{k^{3}}\exp\left(-0.1\sqrt{a_{p,1}^{(t)}/k}\right)\pi_{1,1}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:tiny_pi_1_2h+1_up_phase1.2}}{\geq}\frac{1+o(1)}{k^{3}}\exp\left(0.3a_{p,2}^{(t)}-0.1\sqrt{a_{p,1}^{(t)}/k}\right)\sum_{h=1}^{k-1}\frac{k-h}{k}\pi_{1,2h+1}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})

\displaystyle\geq k^{3}\sum_{h=1}^{k-1}\frac{k-h}{k}\pi_{1,2h+1}^{(t)}(\texttt{up}),

(388)

where the last inequality follows from (
69a
) and (
72h
), and the third line uses (
74e
), which is proven above to be hold also for all
t\in[T_{1,3}^{\prime},T_{1,3}]
. Therefore, by (
336b
) and (
A.3.5
), we have for all
t\in[T_{1,3}^{\prime},s-1]
:

\displaystyle\delta a_{q,\times}^{(t)}=(1+o(1))\sum_{h=1}^{k-1}\frac{k-h}{k}\left(1-\pi_{1,2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\right)>0,

(389)

and by the penultimate line of (
A.3.5
), (
389
) and our choice of
T_{1,3}^{\prime}
we know that

\displaystyle\forall t\in[T_{1,3}^{\prime},s-1]:\quad\sum_{h=1}^{k-1}\frac{k-h}{k}\pi_{1,2h+1}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\leq\exp\left(-0.1a_{p,2}^{(t)}\right)\delta a_{q,\times}^{(t)}.

(390)

We next show (
74c
) holds at step
T_{1,3}
. To do so,
we first prove by contradiction that there exists
t_{0}\in[T_{1/2},T_{1,3}]
such that

\displaystyle r_{\texttt{down}_{\times}}^{(t)}\left(a_{p,1}^{(t)}+a_{p,2}^{(t)}\right)+0.05\sqrt{a_{p,1}^{(t)}/k}+5\ln k

\displaystyle\geq\min\left\{a_{q,\times}^{(t)}-\mu_{2}^{(t)},a_{q,\times}^{(t)}-\mu_{2(k-1)}^{(t)}\right\}.

(391)

Suppose otherwise, i.e., for all
t\in[T_{1/2},T_{1,3}]
, we have

\displaystyle r_{\texttt{down}_{\times}}^{(t)}\left(a_{p,1}^{(t)}+a_{p,2}^{(t)}\right)+0.05\sqrt{a_{p,1}^{(t)}/k}+5\ln k

\displaystyle<\min\left\{a_{q,\times}^{(t)}-\mu_{2}^{(t)},a_{q,\times}^{(t)}-\mu_{2(k-1)}^{(t)}\right\},

(392)

then

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{k-1}\frac{k-h}{k}\left(1-\pi_{1,2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\right)

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:sum_1-pi_2h_up_ub}}{\leq}\frac{1+o(1)}{2}k^{3}\exp\left(-\min\left\{a_{q,\times}^{(s-1)}-\mu_{2}^{(s-1)},a_{q,\times}^{(s-1)}-\mu_{2(k-1)}^{(s-1)}\right\}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:a_q_2>a_q_x_bound_T_13_contradiction}}{\leq}\frac{1+o(1)}{2k^{2}}\exp\left(-r_{\texttt{down}_{\times}}^{(t)}\left(a_{p,1}^{(t)}+a_{p,2}^{(t)}\right)-0.05\sqrt{a_{p,1}^{(t)}/k}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:sum_pi_1_2h+1_down_times_bound_phase1.2}}{\leq}\frac{1+o(1)}{2k^{2}}\exp\left(-0.05\sqrt{a_{p,1}^{(t)}/k}\right)\sum_{h=1}^{k-1}\frac{k-h}{k}\pi_{1,2h+1}^{(t)}(\texttt{down}_{\times}),

(393)

which together with (
336f
), (
336b
) gives

\displaystyle\forall t\in[T_{1/2},T_{1,3}]:\quad\delta a_{p,1}^{(t)}\geq 2(1+o(1))k\exp\left(0.05\sqrt{a_{p,1}^{(t)}/k}\right)\delta a_{q,\times}^{(t)}.

(394)

Combining (
394
), (
379
) and (
379
), we can see that

\displaystyle a_{p,1}^{(T_{1,3})}>a_{p,1}^{(T_{1,3})}-a_{p,1}^{(T_{1/2})}\geq 2(1+o(1))k\exp\left(0.05\sqrt{a_{p,1}^{(T_{1,3})}/k}\right)\left(a_{q,\times}^{(T_{1,3})}-a_{q,\times}^{(T_{1/2})}\right)\gg k\ln k.

(395)

But by (
72b
) and (
72h
) we have

\displaystyle a_{p,1}^{(T_{1,3})}\lesssim ka_{q,\times}^{(T_{1,3})}\lesssim k\ln k.

(396)

The above two relations contradict each other. Thus there exists
t_{0}\in[T_{1/2},T_{1,3}]
such that (
391
) holds at step
t_{0}
. If
t_{0}=T_{1,3}
, then we are done. Otherwise, we can choose
t_{0}\in[T_{1/2},T_{1,3}]
to be the biggest step such that (
391
) holds.
We next show that (
74c
) holds for all
t\in[t_{0},T_{1,3}]
. Suppose otherwise, then let
t_{1}\in[t_{0}+1,T_{1,3}]
by the smallest step such that (
74c
) does not hold at step
t_{1}
, then by Induction Hypothesis II we have

\displaystyle\frac{a_{p,1}^{(t_{1})}}{2k}+0.1\sqrt{a_{p,1}^{(t_{1})}/k}+5\ln k+o(1)

\displaystyle=r_{\texttt{down}_{\times}}^{(t_{1})}\left(a_{p,1}^{(t_{1})}+a_{p,2}^{(t_{1})}\right)+0.1\sqrt{a_{p,1}^{(t_{1})}/k}+5\ln k

\displaystyle<\min\left\{a_{q,\times}^{(t_{1})}-\mu_{2}^{(t_{1})},a_{q,\times}^{(t_{1})}-\mu_{2(k-1)}^{(t_{1})}\right\}=a_{q,\times}^{(t_{1})}-\frac{a_{p,1}^{(t_{1})}}{2k}+o(1).

(397)

By our choice of
t_{0}
, gradient update rule and Induction Hypothesis II, we have for all
t\in[t_{0},t_{1}-1]
:

\displaystyle\frac{a_{p,1}^{(t)}}{2k}+0.05\sqrt{a_{p,1}^{(t)}/k}+5\ln k+o(1)\leq a_{q,\times}^{(t)}-\frac{a_{p,1}^{(t)}}{2k}.

(398)

and thus similar as (
394
), we can compute that

\displaystyle\forall t\in[t_{0},t_{1}-1]:\quad\delta a_{p,1}^{(t)}\gtrsim k\exp\left(0.05\sqrt{a_{p,1}^{(t)}/k}\right)\delta a_{q,\times}^{(t)}.

(399)

Then

\displaystyle\frac{a_{p,1}^{(t_{1})}}{2k}+0.05\sqrt{a_{p,1}^{(t_{1})}/k}+5\ln k-\left(a_{q,\times}^{(t_{1})}-\frac{a_{p,1}^{(t_{1})}}{2k}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:a_q_2>a_q_x_bound_T_13_tight}}{\geq}o(1)+\eta\sum_{t=t_{0}}^{t_{1}-1}\left(\frac{1}{k}\delta a_{p,1}^{(t)}-\delta a_{q,\times}^{(t)}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:delta_a_p_1_t_1-1_contradiction_T_13}}{\gtrsim}\eta\sum_{t=t_{0}}^{t_{1}-1}\left(\exp\left(0.05\sqrt{a_{p,1}^{(t)}/k}\right)-1\right)\delta a_{q,\times}^{(t)}+o(1)

\displaystyle\gtrsim\eta\left(\exp\left(0.05\sqrt{a_{p,1}^{(t_{0})}/k}\right)-1\right)\sum_{t=t_{0}}^{t_{1}-1}\delta a_{q,\times}^{(t)}+o(1)\geq o(1).

(400)

This together with our choice of
T_{1/2}
suggests

\displaystyle\frac{a_{p,1}^{(t_{1})}}{2k}+0.1\sqrt{a_{p,1}^{(t_{1})}/k}+5\ln k\geq a_{q,\times}^{(t_{1})}-\frac{a_{p,1}^{(t_{1})}}{2k}+0.05\sqrt{a_{p,1}^{(t_{1})}/k}+o(1),

(401)

which contradicts (
A.3.5
). Therefore, we have shown that (
74c
) holds for all
t\in[t_{0},T_{1,3}]
.

We next show (
74d
) holds at step
T_{1,3}
. First, if
a_{q,0}^{(T_{1,3})}\leq 12\ln k
, then by our choice of
T_{1,3}
, (
72h
), (
72f
) and the fact that
r_{\texttt{down}_{\times}}^{(t)}\geq\frac{1}{2k}
for all
t\in[T_{1/2},T_{1,3}]
guaranteed by Induction Hypothesis II, we have (
74d
) holds at step
T_{1,3}
. Below we assume

\displaystyle a_{q,0}^{(T_{1,3})}>12\ln k.

(402)

We first show that there exists
t_{0}\in[T_{1/2},T_{1,3}]
such that

\displaystyle r_{\texttt{down}_{\times}}^{(t_{0})}\left(a_{p,1}^{(t_{0})}+a_{p,2}^{(t_{0})}\right)+2.05\ln k\geq\left(1+\frac{1}{k}\right)a_{q,0}^{(t_{0})}-\frac{a_{p,0}^{(t_{0})}}{k}.

(403)

Suppose for all
t\in[T_{1/2},T_{1,3}]
, we have

\displaystyle r_{\texttt{down}_{\times}}^{(t)}\left(a_{p,1}^{(t)}+a_{p,2}^{(t)}\right)+2.05\ln k<\left(1+\frac{1}{k}\right)a_{q,0}^{(t)}-\frac{a_{p,0}^{(t)}}{k},

(404)

then for all
t\in[T_{1/2},T_{1,3}]
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{k-1}\frac{k-h}{k}\pi_{1,2h+1}^{(t)}(\texttt{down}_{\times})

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:sum_pi_1_2h+1_down_times_bound_phase1.2}}{\geq}\frac{1+o(1)}{k}\exp\left(-r_{\texttt{down}_{\times}}^{(t)}\left(a_{p,1}^{(t)}+a_{p,2}^{(t)}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\geq\left(1+o(1)\right)k^{2.05}\frac{\exp\left(-\left(1+\frac{1}{k}\right)a_{q,0}^{(t)}+\frac{a_{p,0}^{(t)}}{k}\right)}{k}\overset{\eqref{eq:pi_1_1_up_phase1.2}}{=}k^{2.05}\pi_{1,1}^{(t)}(\texttt{up}).

(405)

This combined with (
336f
) and (
336a
) suggests

\displaystyle\delta a_{p,1}^{(t)}\geq(1+o(1))k^{1.05}\pi_{1,1}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\geq(1+o(1))k^{1.05}\delta a_{q,0}^{(t)}.

(406)

Note that by (
66a
) and (
66f
) we know that

\displaystyle a_{q,0}^{(T_{1/2})}\leq 10.2\ln k+C/2,

(407)

Therefore, by (
407
), (
402
) and (
406
), we have

\displaystyle a_{p,1}^{(T_{1,3})}>a_{p,1}^{(T_{1,3})}-a_{p,1}^{(T_{1/2})}\gtrsim k^{1.05}\left(a_{q,0}^{(T_{1,3})}-a_{q,0}^{(T_{1/2})}\right)\gtrsim k^{1.05}\ln k,

(408)

which contradicts our choice of
T_{1,3}
(c.f. (
69a
)). Therefore, there exists
t_{0}\in[T_{1/2},T_{1,3}]
such that (
403
) holds. Then similar as how we show (
74c
) or (
74e
) holds at step
T_{1,3}
above, building upon (
403
), we can show by contradiction that (
74d
) holds at step
T_{1,3}
.

We now prove that (
74b
) holds at step
T_{1,3}
.
We first show that there exists
t_{0}\in[T_{1,3}^{\prime},T_{1,3}]
such that

\displaystyle\min\left\{a_{q,\times}^{(t_{0})}-\mu_{2}^{(t_{0})}-\ln k,a_{q,\times}^{(t_{0})}-\mu_{2(k-1)}^{(t_{0})}+\ln k\right\}+\frac{2}{k}a_{q,0}^{(t_{0})}\geq r_{\texttt{down}_{\times}}^{(t_{0})}\left(a_{p,1}^{(t_{0})}+a_{p,2}^{(t_{0})}\right)+1.95\ln k.

(409)

Suppose for all
t\in[T_{1,3}^{\prime},T_{1,3}]
, we have

\displaystyle\min\left\{a_{q,\times}^{(t)}-\mu_{2}^{(t)}-\ln k,a_{q,\times}^{(t)}-\mu_{2(k-1)}^{(t)}+\ln k\right\}+\frac{2}{k}a_{q,0}^{(t)}<r_{\texttt{down}_{\times}}^{(t)}\left(a_{p,1}^{(t)}+a_{p,2}^{(t)}\right)+1.95\ln k.

(410)

Then for all
t\in[T_{1,3}^{\prime},T_{1,3}]
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{k-1}\frac{k-h}{k}\left(1-\pi_{1,2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\right)

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:sum_1-pi_2h_up_lb}}{\geq}(1+o(1))\exp\left(-\min\left\{a_{q,\times}^{(t)}-\mu_{2}^{(t)}-\ln k,a_{q,\times}^{(t)}-\mu_{2(k-1)}^{(t)}+\ln k\right\}\right)

\displaystyle\geq(1+o(1))\exp\left(-r_{\texttt{down}_{\times}}^{(t)}\left(a_{p,1}^{(t)}+a_{p,2}^{(t)}\right)-1.95\ln k+\frac{2}{k}a_{q,0}^{(t)}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:sum_pi_1_2h+1_down_times_bound_phase1.2}}{\geq}\frac{2+o(1)}{k^{1.95}}\sum_{h=1}^{k-1}\frac{k-h}{k}\pi_{1,2h+1}^{(t)}(\texttt{down}_{\times}),

(411)

and by (
A.3.5
) our choice of
T_{1,3}^{\prime}
we have

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{k-1}\frac{k-h}{k}\pi_{1,2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{down}_{\times}^{1})=o\left(\frac{1}{k^{1.95}}\right)\sum_{h=1}^{k-1}\frac{k-h}{k}\left(1-\pi_{1,2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\right).

(412)

the above two expressions and (
336f
), (
389
) we have for all
t\in[T_{1,3}^{\prime},T_{1,3}]
:

\displaystyle\delta a_{p,1}^{(t)}\leq(2+o(1))k^{0.95}\delta a_{q,\times}^{(t)},

(413)

from which and our choice of
T_{1,3},T_{1,3}^{\prime}
we deduce

\displaystyle a_{q,\times}^{(T_{1,3})}>a_{q,\times}^{(T_{1,3})}-a_{q,\times}^{(T_{1,3}^{\prime})}\gtrsim k^{-0.95}\left(a_{p,1}^{(T_{1,3})}-a_{p,1}^{(T_{1,3}^{\prime})}\right)\gtrsim k^{0.05}\ln k,

(414)

which contradicts that fact that
a_{q,\times}^{(T_{1,3})}\asymp\ln k
guaranteed by (
72b
), (
72h
) and our choice of
T_{1,3}
. Therefore, there exists
t_{0}\in[T_{1,3}^{\prime},T_{1,3}]
such that (
409
) holds. Then similar as how we show (
74c
) or (
74e
) holds at step
T_{1,3}
above, building upon (
409
), we can show by contradiction that (
74b
) holds at step
T_{1,3}
.

When
T_{1,3}+1\leq s\leq T_{1,4}
.

We first show relations between
a_{b,0}^{(s)}
,
a_{b,1}^{(s)}
and
a_{b,2}^{(s)}
. First note that (
A.3.5
), (
389
) and (
390
) still hold here, and we’ll frequently use them in the subsequent proof.

First, from (
390
) and (
336c
) and (
72b
) we know that for all
t\in[T_{1,3},s-1]
:

\displaystyle\delta e^{2a_{c,0}^{(t)}}\lesssim\frac{a_{p,2}^{(t)}}{\exp\left(0.1a_{p,2}^{(t)}\right)}\delta a_{q,\times}^{(t)}.

(415)

Then (i) if
a_{p,1}^{(s)}\leq k^{2}
, then by (
415
), (
72f
), (
72b
) and our choice of
T_{1,3}
we have

\displaystyle e^{2a_{c,0}^{(s)}}-e^{2a_{c,0}^{(T_{1,3})}}\lesssim\frac{21\ln k}{\exp\left(0.1\times 21\ln k\right)}k\lesssim\frac{\ln k}{k^{1.1}}.

(416)

And thus by (
373
) and the above relation we know that if
a_{p,1}^{(s)}\leq k^{2}
:

\displaystyle e^{2a_{c,0}^{(s)}}\lesssim\ln^{2}k.

(417)

(ii) if
a_{p,1}^{(s)}>k^{2}
, then we can choose
t_{0}
such that

\displaystyle t_{0}\coloneqq\sup\left\{t\in[T_{1,3},s-1]:a_{p,1}^{(t)}=k^{2}\right\}.

(418)

Then above we have shown

\displaystyle e^{2a_{c,0}^{(t_{0})}}-e^{2a_{c,0}^{(T_{1,3})}}\lesssim\frac{\ln k}{k^{1.1}}.

(419)

From
(
415
), (
74a
) and (
72h
), (
72b
) we deduce

\displaystyle e^{2a_{c,0}^{(s)}}-e^{2a_{c,0}^{(t_{0})}}\lesssim\frac{k}{\exp\left(0.1k\right)}k^{5/4}\sqrt{N}=o(1).

(420)

By the two relations above and (
373
) we know that

\displaystyle e^{2a_{c,0}^{(s)}}\lesssim\ln^{2}k.

(421)

Therefore, from (
421
) and (
343
), (
344
) we know that
(
72c
) holds at step
s
.

By (
338
) and Induction Hypothesis II we have for any
t\in[T_{1/2},s-1]
:

\displaystyle I_{h,1}^{(t)}

\displaystyle=\left(1+O\left(\frac{1}{k}\right)\right)\left(1-hr_{\texttt{down},h,1}^{(t)}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\cdot\left(ka_{p,2}^{(t)}\frac{he^{a_{b,1}^{(t)}}}{e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}+he^{a_{b,1}^{(t)}}+he^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}\pi_{1,2h+1}^{(t)}(\texttt{down}_{\times})+hr_{\texttt{down},h,1}^{(t)}a_{p,2}^{(t)}\pi_{1,2h+1}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\right).

(422)

From (
75c
) and (
72a
) in Induction Hypothesis II we know that

\displaystyle\forall t\in[T_{1/2},s-1]:\quad 0<a_{b,1}^{(t)}\lesssim\ln k.

(423)

From our choice of

in (

), (

) and (

\displaystyle\forall t\in[T_{1/2},s-1]:\quad|a_{b,0}^{(t)}|\lesssim\ln k.

Therefore, by the above two expressions and (

) we know that

\displaystyle\forall t\in[T_{1/2},s-1]:\quad|a_{b,2}^{(t)}|\lesssim\frac{\ln k}{N}.

(425)

By our choice of
T_{1,4}
in (
69b
), (
74f
) in Induction Hypothesis II and (
425
) we have

\displaystyle 1-hr_{\texttt{down},h,1}^{(t)}\overset{\eqref{eq:r_down_h_phase1.2}}{=}\frac{e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}+he^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}{e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}+he^{a_{b,1}^{(t)}}+he^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}\lesssim\frac{N}{\left(a_{p,1}^{(t)}\right)^{2}\ln k}.

(426)

Combining (
A.3.5
), (

), (

) and (

) we obtain that for all
t\in[T_{1,3},s-1]
:

\displaystyle 0<\delta a_{b,1}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{N}{\left(a_{p,1}^{(t)}\right)^{2}\ln k}\left(\frac{k^{2}a_{p,2}^{(t)}}{N}\delta a_{p,1}^{(t)}+\frac{a_{p,2}^{(t)}}{N\exp\left(0.1a_{p,2}^{(t)}\right)}\delta a_{q,\times}^{(t)}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:a_p_2_bound_phase_1.2}}{\lesssim}\frac{1}{a_{p,2}^{(t)}\ln k}\delta a_{p,1}^{(t)}+\frac{1}{k^{2}\ln ka_{p,2}^{(t)}\exp\left(0.1a_{p,2}^{(t)}\right)}\delta a_{q,\times}^{(t)}.

(427)

Similar as (
183
), we define

\displaystyle\forall i\in\{0,1,2\}:\quad\delta e^{a_{b,i}^{(t)}}\coloneqq\frac{e^{a_{b,i}^{(t+1)}}-e^{a_{b,i}^{(t)}}}{\eta}=\frac{e^{a_{b,i}^{(t)}}}{\eta}\left(e^{\eta\delta a_{b,i}^{(t)}}-1\right)=(1+o(1))e^{a_{b,i}^{(t)}}\delta a_{b,i}^{(t)}.

(428)

Then by (
336i
), (
337
), (
425
), (
72h
) and (

) we know that for all
t\in[T_{1/2},s-1]
:

\displaystyle\delta e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}\leq(1+o(1))\frac{a_{p,1}^{(t)}}{N}\delta a_{q,\times}^{(t)},

(429)

which suggests

\displaystyle e^{a_{b,0}^{(s-1)}}-e^{a_{b,0}^{(T_{1,3})}}\leq\frac{1+o(1)}{N}a_{p,1}^{(s-1)}a_{q,\times}^{(s-1)}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{Nk}\left(a_{p,1}^{(s-1)}\right)^{2},

(430)

where the last inequality follows from (
72h
) and (

). Thus by the above expression and (

), there exists constant

such that

\displaystyle e^{a_{b,0}^{(s-1)}}\leq 1+\frac{c}{Nk}\left(a_{p,1}^{(s-1)}\right)^{2}.

(431)

And by (
A.3.5
) we have for all
t\in[T_{1/2},s-1]
and
h\in[k-1]
:

\displaystyle e^{a_{b,1}^{(t)}}I_{h,1}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\geq\left(1+O\left(\frac{1}{k}\right)\right)\frac{e^{a_{b,1}^{(t)}}\left(e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}+he^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}\right)}{e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}+he^{a_{b,1}^{(t)}}+he^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}a_{p,1}^{(t)}\cdot\frac{he^{a_{b,1}^{(t)}}}{e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}+he^{a_{b,1}^{(t)}}+he^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}\pi_{1,2h+1}^{(t)}(\texttt{down}_{\times})

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:a_b_2_approx_0_t<=T_1_4}}{\geq}\left(1+O\left(\frac{1}{k}\right)\right)\frac{he^{a_{b,1}^{(t)}}}{e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}+he^{a_{b,1}^{(t)}}+he^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}a_{p,1}^{(t)}\cdot\frac{he^{a_{b,1}^{(t)}}}{e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}+he^{a_{b,1}^{(t)}}+he^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}\pi_{1,2h+1}^{(t)}(\texttt{down}_{\times})

\displaystyle\geq\left(1+O\left(\frac{1}{k}\right)\right)\frac{a_{p,1}^{(t)}}{3}\Bigg(\frac{he^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}{e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}+(2h-1)e^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}\pi_{1,2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{down}_{\times}^{1})

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{he^{a_{b,1}^{(t)}}}{e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}+he^{a_{b,1}^{(t)}}+he^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}\pi_{1,2h+1}^{(t)}(\texttt{down}_{\times})-\frac{he^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}{e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}+(2h-1)e^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}\pi_{1,2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{down}_{\times}^{1})\Bigg),

where the first inequality uses (
72h
), and the third inequality uses (

) in Induction Hypothesis II and (
425
).
By (

), the first relation in (
A.3.5
) and (

) we know that

\displaystyle\forall h\in[k-1]:\quad\pi_{1,2h}^{(s-1)}(\texttt{down}_{\times}^{1})

\displaystyle\leq\exp\left(-r_{\texttt{up},h,2}^{(s-1)}\left(a_{p,1}^{(s-1)}+a_{p,2}^{(s-1)}\right)\right)\left(1-\pi_{1,2h}^{(s-1)}(\texttt{up})\right)

\displaystyle\leq\exp\left(-\frac{a_{p,1}^{(s-1)}+a_{p,2}^{(s-1)}}{2h+\frac{c}{Nk}\left(a_{p,1}^{(s-1)}\right)^{2}}\right)\left(1-\pi_{1,2h}^{(s-1)}(\texttt{up})\right)

\displaystyle\leq\exp\left(-\left(1+O\left(\frac{1}{k}\right)\right)\frac{1}{2\frac{k}{a_{p,1}^{(s-1)}}+\frac{c}{Nk}a_{p,1}^{(s-1)}}\right)\left(1-\pi_{1,2h}^{(s-1)}(\texttt{up})\right).

(433)

By our choice of
T_{1,3}
, (
74a
) and the fact that
a_{p,1}^{(t)}
strictly increases, we have

\displaystyle\forall t\in[T_{1,3},s-1]:\quad 21k\ln k\leq a_{p,1}^{(t)}\leq k^{9/4}\sqrt{N}.

(434)

Combining the above two inequalities, we have
(i) if
a_{p,1}^{(s-1)}\leq k^{2}
, then

\displaystyle\pi_{1,2h}^{(s-1)}(\texttt{down}_{\times}^{1})\leq\frac{1}{k^{10}}\left(1-\pi_{1,2h}^{(s-1)}(\texttt{up})\right)\leq\frac{1-\pi_{1,2h}^{(s-1)}(\texttt{up})}{k^{8}a_{p,1}^{(s-1)}},

(435)

and (ii) if
a_{p,1}^{(s-1)}\geq k^{2}
, then

\displaystyle\pi_{1,2h}^{(s-1)}(\texttt{down}_{\times}^{1})\leq e^{-k}\left(1-\pi_{1,2h}^{(s-1)}(\texttt{up})\right)\overset{\eqref{eq:a_p_1_ub_T_1_4}}{\leq}\frac{1-\pi_{1,2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})}{k^{8}a_{p,1}^{(s-1)}}.

(436)

Thereafter, we have

\displaystyle\forall h\in[k-1],\,\,t\in[T_{1/2},s-1]:\quad\pi_{1,2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{down}_{\times}^{1})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{k^{8}a_{p,1}^{(t)}}\left(1-\pi_{1,2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\right).

(437)

By (

), (

), (

), (
437
) and (

) we have for all
t\in[T_{1,3},s-1]

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1+o(1)}{N}\left(\frac{ka_{p,1}^{(t)}}{3}\delta a_{p,1}^{(t)}-\frac{1}{3k^{8}}\delta a_{q,\times}^{(t)}\right).

This indicates that (

) holds at step

On the other hand, similar as how we obtain (
A.3.5
), from (

,

\displaystyle\delta e^{a_{b,1}^{(t)}}

\displaystyle\leq(1+o(1))\frac{e^{a_{b,1}^{(t)}}\left(e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}+he^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}\right)}{e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}+he^{a_{b,1}^{(t)}}+he^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}\left(\frac{k^{2}a_{p,2}^{(t)}}{N}\delta a_{p,1}^{(t)}+\frac{a_{p,2}^{(t)}}{N\exp\left(0.1a_{p,2}^{(t)}\right)}\delta a_{q,\times}^{(t)}\right)

\displaystyle\leq(1+o(1))\frac{k}{\ln k}\left(\frac{k^{2}a_{p,2}^{(t)}}{N}\delta a_{p,1}^{(t)}+\frac{a_{p,2}^{(t)}}{Nk^{2}}\delta a_{q,\times}^{(t)}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:a_p_2_bound_phase_1.2}}{=}(1+o(1))\frac{k}{\ln k}\left(\frac{ka_{p,1}^{(t)}}{N}\delta a_{p,1}^{(t)}+\frac{a_{p,1}^{(t)}}{Nk^{3}}\delta a_{q,\times}^{(t)}\right),

\displaystyle\leq(1+o(1))\frac{k^{2}}{N\ln k}\left(a_{p,1}^{(s)}\right)^{2}\overset{\eqref{eq:a_p_1_ub_T_1_4}}{\lesssim}\frac{k^{6.5}}{\ln k},

where the second inequality follows from (

) and (

) holds at step

.

We now show (

) holds at step
s
. First, when

, by (

) and (

) we know that

\displaystyle e^{a_{b,0}^{(T_{1,3})}}-1<0<\frac{e^{a_{b,1}^{(T_{1,3})}}-1}{k^{2}},

) holds at step

. If

\displaystyle e^{a_{b,1}^{(s)}}-1\geq e^{a_{b,1}^{(s)}}-e^{a_{b,1}^{(t^{\prime})}}

\displaystyle\gtrsim\frac{k}{N}\left(a_{p,1}^{(s)}\right)^{2}\overset{\eqref{eq:e_a_b_0_bound_T_1_4}}{\gtrsim}k^{2}\left(e^{a_{b,0}^{(s)}}-1\right).

(441)

This shows (
74f
) holds at step
s
.

Moreover, by (

), (

) and Induction Hypothesis II we have

\displaystyle I_{h,0}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\left(1+O\left(\frac{1}{k}\right)\right)r_{\texttt{up},h,0}^{(t)}r_{\texttt{up},h,2}^{(t)}ha_{p,2}^{(t)}\left(1-\pi_{1,2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad-\left(1+O\left(\frac{1}{k}\right)\right)hr_{\texttt{down},h,0}^{(t)}r_{\texttt{down},h,1}^{(t)}\left(a_{p,1}^{(t)}\pi_{1,2h+1}^{(t)}(\texttt{down}_{\times})+a_{p,2}^{(t)}\pi_{1,2h+1}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\right).

(442)

Therefore, by (
389
) and (

) we have for all

\displaystyle\delta a_{b,0}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1+o(1)}{4N}a_{p,2}^{(t)}\delta a_{q,\times}^{(t)},

where we use the fact that
hr_{\texttt{up},h,2}^{(t)}\leq 1-r_{\texttt{up},h,0}^{(t)}
, and
x(1-x)\leq\frac{1}{4}
for any
x\in\mathbb{R}
.
And by (
336f
) and (

) we have for all
t\in[T_{1,3},s-1]
:

\displaystyle-\delta a_{b,0}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\leq(1+o(1))r_{\texttt{down},h,0}^{(t)}\left(\frac{k^{2}a_{p,2}^{(t)}}{N}\delta a_{p,1}^{(t)}+\frac{a_{p,2}^{(t)}}{N\exp\left(0.1a_{p,2}^{(t)}\right)}\delta a_{q,\times}^{(t)}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{a_{p,2}^{(t)}\ln k}\delta a_{p,1}^{(t)}+\frac{1}{k^{2}\ln ka_{p,2}^{(t)}\exp\left(0.1a_{p,2}^{(t)}\right)}\delta a_{q,\times}^{(t)},

We next show (
74g
) holds at step
s
. First we have

\displaystyle\delta e^{-a_{b,0}^{(t)}}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\frac{e^{-a_{b,0}^{(t+1)}}-e^{-a_{b,0}^{(t)}}}{\eta}

\displaystyle=(1+o(1))e^{-a_{b,0}^{(t)}}\left(-\delta a_{b,0}^{(t)}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:I_h_0<T_14}}{=}\frac{1+o(1)}{N}\sum_{h=1}^{k-1}\frac{k-h}{k}\frac{he^{a_{b,1}^{(t)}}}{\left(e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}+he^{a_{b,1}^{(t)}}+he^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}\right)^{2}}\left(a_{p,1}^{(t)}\pi_{1,2h+1}^{(t)}(\texttt{down}_{\times})+a_{p,2}^{(t)}\pi_{1,2h+1}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\right)

\displaystyle\qquad-\frac{1+o(1)}{N}\sum_{h=1}^{k-1}\frac{k-h}{k}\frac{he^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}{\left(e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}+(2h-1)e^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}\right)^{2}}a_{p,2}^{(t)}\left(1-\pi_{1,2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\right).

Thus our choice of

, (

), (

), (

) and (

) we have

\displaystyle\delta e^{-a_{b,0}^{(t)}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1+o(1)}{N}\frac{1}{e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}+e^{a_{b,1}^{(t)}}+1}\sum_{h=1}^{k-1}\frac{k-h}{k}\frac{he^{a_{b,1}^{(t)}}}{e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}+he^{a_{b,1}^{(t)}}+he^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}\left(a_{p,1}^{(t)}\pi_{1,2h+1}^{(t)}(\texttt{down}_{\times})+a_{p,2}^{(t)}\pi_{1,2h+1}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\right)

\displaystyle\qquad-\frac{1+o(1)}{N}\left(\frac{\ln k}{k}\right)^{2}\sum_{h=1}^{k-1}\frac{k-h}{k}a_{p,2}^{(t)}\left(1-\pi_{1,2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1+o(1)}{N}\frac{1}{e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}+e^{a_{b,1}^{(t)}}+1}\left(ka_{p,1}^{(t)}\delta a_{p,1}^{(t)}+\frac{a_{p,2}^{(t)}}{\exp\left(0.1a_{p,2}^{(t)}\right)}\delta a_{q,\times}^{(t)}\right)-\frac{1+o(1)}{N}\left(\frac{\ln k}{k}\right)^{2}a_{p,2}^{(t)}\delta a_{q,\times}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:a_p_2_bound_phase_1.2}}{\leq}\frac{1+o(1)}{N}\frac{ka_{p,1}^{(t)}}{e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}+e^{a_{b,1}^{(t)}}+1}\delta a_{p,1}^{(t)}-\frac{1+o(1)}{N}\frac{\ln^{2}k}{k^{3}}a_{p,1}^{(t)}\delta a_{q,\times}^{(t)}.

(447)

Define

\displaystyle t_{i}\coloneqq\inf\left\{t\in[T_{1,3},s-1]:e^{a_{b,1}^{(t)}}\geq k^{i/2}\right\},\,\,\forall i\in{\mathbb{N}}.

(448)

then by (

) we know that
t_{0}=T_{1,3}
.
Comparing (

) and (

), we have for any

,

\displaystyle\text{If }t_{i}\leq t\leq t_{i+1}:\quad\delta e^{-a_{b,0}^{(t)}}\lesssim k^{-i/2}\delta e^{a_{b,1}^{(t)}},

Now we show (

with some constant

\displaystyle e^{-a_{b,0}^{(s)}}-e^{-a_{b,0}^{(s_{0})}}\leq-\frac{1+o(1)}{N}\frac{\ln^{2}k}{k^{3}}a_{p,1}^{(s)}\left(a_{q,\times}^{(s)}-a_{q,\times}^{(s_{0})}\right)+(1+o(1))\frac{c}{N}\frac{\ln k}{k^{4.5}}a_{p,1}^{(s)}\left(a_{p,1}^{(s)}-a_{p,1}^{(s_{0})}\right).

Combining the above two expressions, we have

\displaystyle e^{-a_{b,0}^{(s)}}-e^{-a_{b,0}^{(s_{0})}}\leq-\frac{1+o(1)}{N}\frac{\ln^{2}k}{k^{3}}a_{p,1}^{(s)}a_{q,\times}^{(s)}\overset{\eqref{eq:a_p_1_s_ub_contradiction}}{\lesssim}-\frac{1}{N}\frac{\ln^{2}k}{k^{4}}\left(k^{9/4}\sqrt{N}\right)^{2}=\sqrt{k}\ln^{2}k,

) suggests there exists

) holds at step

.

In addition, by (

), (

), (

for any

, which implies (

) holds at step

.

On the other hand, if

, then (

) suggests

\displaystyle\forall t\in[T_{1,3},T_{1,4}-1]:\quad-\delta e^{-a_{b,0}^{(t)}}\leq\frac{1+o(1)}{N}\frac{\ln^{2}k}{k^{3}}a_{p,1}^{(t)}\delta a_{q,\times}^{(t)},

and thus

\displaystyle\frac{1+o(1)}{N}\frac{\ln^{2}k}{k^{3}}a_{p,1}^{(T_{1,4})}\left(a_{q,\times}^{(T_{1,4})}-a_{q,\times}^{(T_{1,3})}\right)\geq\eta\sum_{t=T_{1,3}}^{T_{1,4}-1}\left(-\delta e^{-a_{b,0}^{(t)}}\right)=e^{-a_{b,0}^{(T_{1,3})}}-e^{-a_{b,0}^{(T_{1,4})}}=1+o(1),

where the last relation follows from our choice of
T_{1,4}
and (

). From the above inequality and (

), (

) we deduce

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\frac{\ln^{2}k}{k^{4}}\left(a_{p,1}^{(T_{1,4})}\right)^{2}\gtrsim 1,

from which we can see that

\displaystyle a_{p,1}^{(T_{1,4})}\gtrsim\frac{k^{2}\sqrt{N}}{\ln k}.

(460)

By (
460
) and (
74f
) we have

\displaystyle e^{a_{b,1}^{(T_{1,4})}}\gtrsim\frac{k^{5}}{\ln^{2}k}.

(461)

To show (

), (

), (

), (

) in Lemma

, below we first bound the gradients of

\displaystyle=-r_{\texttt{up},h,0}^{(t+1)}\frac{\delta a_{p,0}^{(t)}}{k}-\frac{r_{\texttt{up},h,0}^{(t+1)}-r_{\texttt{up},h,0}^{(t)}}{\eta}\frac{a_{p,0}^{(t)}}{k}+r_{\texttt{up},h,2}^{(t+1)}\left(h\delta a_{p,2}^{(t)}+\frac{h-1}{k}\delta a_{p,\texttt{up}}^{(t)}\right)+\frac{r_{\texttt{up},h,2}^{(t+1)}-r_{\texttt{up},h,2}^{(t)}}{\eta}\left(ha_{p,2}^{(t)}+\frac{h-1}{k}a_{p,\texttt{up}}^{(t)}\right),

\displaystyle\delta\left(r_{\texttt{down}_{\times}}^{(t)}\left(a_{p,1}^{(t)}+a_{p,2}^{(t)}\right)\right)\geq\left(1+o(1)\right)\left(\frac{1}{2k}\delta a_{p,1}^{(t)}-\frac{c_{3}}{k^{3/2}}\sum_{h=1}^{k-1}\frac{k-h}{k}r_{\texttt{up},h,0}^{(t)}\left(1-\pi_{1,2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\right)\right)

(502)

for some constant
c_{3}>0
. Now we show that (
74d
) holds at step
s
by contradiction. Suppose otherwise, i.e.,

(503)

By Induction Hypothesis II we have

\displaystyle r_{\texttt{down}_{\times}}^{(s-1)}\left(a_{p,1}^{(s-1)}+a_{p,2}^{(s-1)}\right)+2.1\ln k\geq\left(1+\frac{1}{k}\right)a_{q,0}^{(s-1)}-\frac{a_{p,0}^{(s-1)}}{k}.

Meanwhile, by our update rule and (
503
), we have

\displaystyle r_{\texttt{down}_{\times}}^{(s-1)}\left(a_{p,1}^{(s-1)}+a_{p,2}^{(s-1)}\right)+2.1\ln k\leq\left(1+\frac{1}{k}\right)a_{q,0}^{(s-1)}-\frac{a_{p,0}^{(s-1)}}{k}+1,

and similar as how we obtain (

\displaystyle\delta a_{p,1}^{(s-1)}\gtrsim k^{1.1}\pi_{1,1}^{(s-1)}(\texttt{up})\gtrsim k^{1.1}\delta a_{q,0}^{(s-1)}.

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle r_{\texttt{down}_{\times}}^{(s)}\left(a_{p,1}^{(s)}+a_{p,2}^{(s)}\right)+2.1\ln k-\left(\left(1+\frac{1}{k}\right)a_{q,0}^{(s)}-\frac{a_{p,0}^{(s)}}{k}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:a_p2>a_q_0_bound_T_13_contradiction_1_3}}{\geq}\delta\left(r_{\texttt{down}_{\times}}^{(s-1)}\left(a_{p,1}^{(s-1)}+a_{p,2}^{(s-1)}\right)\right)-\left(1+\frac{1}{k}\right)\delta a_{q,0}^{(s-1)}+\frac{\delta a_{p,0}^{(s-1)}}{k}

\displaystyle\geq\left(1+o(1)\right)\left(\frac{1}{2k}\delta a_{p,1}^{(s-1)}-\frac{c_{3}}{k^{3/2}}\sum_{h=1}^{k-1}\frac{k-h}{k}r_{\texttt{up},h,0}^{(s-1)}\left(1-\pi_{1,2h}^{(s-1)}(\texttt{up})\right)\right)-\left(1+\frac{1}{k}\right)\delta a_{q,0}^{(s-1)}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1+o(1)}{k}\left(-\pi_{1,1}^{(s-1)}(\texttt{up})+\sum_{h=1}^{k-1}\frac{k-h}{k}r_{\texttt{up},h,0}^{(s-1)}\left(1-\pi_{1,2h}^{(s-1)}(\texttt{up})\right)\right)

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:delta_a_p_1_bound_T_13_contradiction_1_2}}{\gtrsim}k^{0.1}\delta a_{q,0}^{(s-1)}>0,

), we have

\displaystyle e^{-a_{b,0}^{(t_{0})}}-e^{-a_{b,0}^{(t_{0}^{\prime})}}

\displaystyle\leq-\frac{3+o(1)}{8C^{2}}\frac{\ln^{2}k}{Nk^{4}}a_{p,1}^{(t_{0}^{\prime})}\left(a_{p,1}^{(t_{0})}-a_{p,1}^{(t_{0}^{\prime})}\right)+\frac{c_{1}+o(1)}{a_{p,1}^{(t_{0}^{\prime})}}\left(a_{p,1}^{(t_{0})}-a_{p,1}^{(t_{0}^{\prime})}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:a_p_1_lb_T_1_5}}{\leq}\left(-\frac{3c_{2}+o(1)}{8C^{2}}+\frac{c_{1}+o(1)}{c_{2}}\right)\frac{\ln k}{\sqrt{N}k^{2}}\left(a_{p,1}^{(t_{0})}-a_{p,1}^{(t_{0}^{\prime})}\right)

(544)

for some constant
c_{2}>0
. From the above inequality we can see that we can set
C
small enough such that

\displaystyle e^{-a_{b,0}^{(t_{0})}}-e^{-a_{b,0}^{(t_{0}^{\prime})}}<0.

(545)

However, from (
533
) we have

\displaystyle e^{-a_{b,0}^{(t_{0})}}-e^{-a_{b,0}^{(t_{0}^{\prime})}}\geq\frac{\ln k}{2Ck}.

(546)

The above two inequalities are contradictory. Therefore, (
532
) must hold. This way we have shown (
75a
).

Below we prove (
75h
) assuming
s\geq s_{0}
. Define

\displaystyle s^{\prime}=\sup\left\{t\in[T_{1,4},s-1]:a_{p,1}^{(t)}\leq C_{1}a_{p,1}^{(s)}\right\}

(547)

for some small enough constant
C_{1}>0
.
We first show there exists
t_{1}\in[s^{\prime},s]
such that

\displaystyle e^{a_{b,0}^{(t_{1})}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{C\left(a_{p,1}^{(t_{1})}\right)^{1-\frac{6}{N-1}}}{Nk}

(548)

for some small enough constant
C>0
.
Suppose this does not hold, i.e., for all
t\in[s^{\prime},s]
, we have

\displaystyle e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}<\frac{C\left(a_{p,1}^{(t)}\right)^{1-\frac{6}{N-1}}}{Nk}.

(549)

Then same as for (

), we have for all
t\in[s^{\prime},s]
and

:

\displaystyle\pi_{1,2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{down}_{\times}^{1})

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\pi_{1,2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\right)\exp\left(-r_{\texttt{up},h,2}^{(t)}\left(a_{p,1}^{(t)}+a_{p,2}^{(t)}\right)\right)=o\left(\frac{1}{k}\right)\left(1-\pi_{1,2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\right).

(550)

Then by (
A.3.5
) we have for all

:

\displaystyle\delta e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1+o(1)}{N}\sum_{h=1}^{k-1}\frac{k-h}{k}\left(r_{\texttt{up},h,0}^{(t)}\right)^{2}he^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}a_{p,2}^{(t)}\left(1-\pi_{1,2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\right)

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1+o(1)}{N}\sum_{h=1}^{k-1}\frac{k-h}{k}\frac{he^{a_{b,1}^{(t)}}e^{2a_{b,0}^{(t)}}}{\left(e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}+he^{a_{b,1}^{(t)}}+he^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}\right)^{2}}\left(a_{p,1}^{(t)}\pi_{1,2h+1}^{(t)}(\texttt{down}_{\times})+a_{p,2}^{(t)}\pi_{1,2h+1}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1+o(1)}{Nk}\left(a_{p,1}^{(t)}\right)^{1-\frac{6}{N-1}}\delta a_{q,\times}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1+o(1)}{N}\frac{e^{2a_{b,0}^{(t)}}}{e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}+e^{a_{b,1}^{(t)}}+e^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}\left(ka_{p,1}^{(t)}\delta a_{p,1}^{(t)}+\frac{a_{p,2}^{(t)}}{\exp\left(0.1a_{p,2}^{(t)}\right)}\delta a_{q,\times}^{(t)}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1+o(1)}{Nk}\left(a_{p,1}^{(t)}\right)^{1-\frac{6}{N-1}}\delta a_{q,\times}^{(t)}-\frac{C^{2}+o(1)}{Nk^{2}}\left(a_{p,1}^{(t)}\right)^{1-\frac{10}{N-1}}\delta a_{p,1}^{(t)},

From (

), (

) we know that there exist constants

,

(553)

We can choose

in (

\displaystyle a_{q,\times}^{(s_{0})}-a_{q,\times}^{(s_{0}^{\prime})}\geq c_{1}\frac{a_{p,1}^{(s_{0})}}{k}-c_{2}\frac{a_{p,1}^{(s_{0}^{\prime})}}{k}\geq c_{1}\frac{a_{p,1}^{(s_{0})}}{2k}.

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1+o(1)}{Nk^{2}}\frac{c_{1}^{2}}{4c_{2}}\left(a_{p,1}^{(s)}\right)^{2-\frac{6}{N-1}}-\frac{C^{2}+o(1)}{Nk^{2}}\left(a_{p,1}^{(s)}\right)^{2-\frac{10}{N-1}}

\displaystyle\gtrsim\frac{1}{Nk^{2}}\left(a_{p,1}^{(s)}\right)^{2-\frac{6}{N-1}}\overset{\eqref{eq:s_0}}{\gg}\frac{\left(a_{p,1}^{(s)}\right)^{1-\frac{6}{N-1}}}{Nk}

(555)

if we set

\displaystyle\geq\left(1+O\left(\frac{1}{k}\right)\right)\frac{\left(a_{p,1}^{(t)}\right)^{2-\frac{6}{N-1}}}{Nk^{2}}\frac{he^{a_{b,1}^{(t)}}}{e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}+he^{a_{b,1}^{(t)}}+he^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}\pi_{1,2h+1}^{(t)}(\texttt{down}_{\times})

\displaystyle\geq\left(1+O\left(\frac{1}{k}\right)\right)\frac{\left(a_{p,1}^{(t)}\right)^{2-\frac{6}{N-1}}}{Nk^{2}}\Bigg(\frac{he^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}{e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}+(2h-1)e^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}\pi_{1,2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{down}_{\times}^{1})

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{he^{a_{b,1}^{(t)}}}{e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}+he^{a_{b,1}^{(t)}}+he^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}\pi_{1,2h+1}^{(t)}(\texttt{down}_{\times})-\frac{he^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}{e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}+(2h-1)e^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}\pi_{1,2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{down}_{\times}^{1})\Bigg),

(570)

(571)

and same as for (

\displaystyle e^{a_{b,1}^{(T_{1})}}\gtrsim\frac{1}{N^{2}k}\left(a_{p,1}^{(T_{1})}\right)^{3-\frac{6}{N-1}}\overset{\eqref{eq:ea_b_1>=ea_b_0_T_15}}{\gtrsim}\frac{1}{N}\left(a_{p,1}^{(T_{1})}\right)^{1-\frac{5}{N}}e^{a_{b,0}^{(T_{1})}}.

(572)

\displaystyle=-\frac{a_{p,0}^{(T_{1})}}{k}+\left(1+O\left(\frac{1}{k}\right)\right)N\left(a_{p,1}^{(T_{1})}\right)^{\frac{6}{N-1}}.

\displaystyle k\epsilon^{1.05}\geq\pi_{1,2k-1}^{(T_{1})}(\texttt{down}_{\times})=\frac{\exp\left(-r_{\texttt{down}_{\times}}^{(T_{1})}\left(a_{p,1}^{(T_{1})}+a_{p,2}^{(T_{1})}\right)\right)}{(k-1)\exp\left(-r_{\texttt{down}_{\times}}^{(T_{1})}\left(a_{p,1}^{(T_{1})}+a_{p,2}^{(T_{1})}\right)\right)+1+o(\epsilon)}.

(574)

By (

), (

) and (

) we have

\displaystyle r_{\texttt{down},\times}^{(T_{1})}=\frac{1}{k-1}+O\left(\frac{1}{k^{2}e^{a_{b,1}^{(T_{1})}}}\right)\overset{\eqref{eq:ea_b_1_lb_T_1}}{=}\frac{1}{k-1}+o\left(\frac{1}{k\ln(1/\epsilon)}\right).

(575)

), we obtain (

).

Finally, we show (
76i
). Specifically, we’ll show that given any tree with depth
l\in\{2,\cdots,L\}
drawn from the balanced training distribution
{\mathcal{P}}_{l}
, under the event that the goal is not at the first
k
leaves
\pi^{(T_{1})}
visits (which happens with probability
1-1/k^{l-1}
), with probabillity
1-o(1)
, the agent will go all the way down to a leaf, and visit all its siblings and go back to its parent node. At this node, with probability
1-o(1)
, the agent will choose to go down again and visit a leaf that’s already been visited, and the task fails.

Below we give the formal proof. All probabilities here are under policy
\pi^{(T_{1})}
and the balanced training distribution
{\mathcal{P}}_{l}
that satisfies (
19
) for a fixed
l\in\{2,3,\cdots,L\}
. We define events

\displaystyle{\mathcal{C}}_{1}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\left\{\text{goal is not at the first $k$ leaves $\pi^{(T_{1})}$ visits, and }a_{h}\in[k],\forall h\in[l]\right\},

(576)

\displaystyle{\mathcal{C}}_{2}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\left\{a_{l}=\texttt{up}\right\},

(577)

and for all
i\in\{2,3,\cdots,k\}
:

\displaystyle{\mathcal{C}}_{2i-1}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\left\{a_{l+2(i-1)}\in[k]\setminus{\mathcal{A}}_{l+2(i-1),\times}\right\},

(578)

\displaystyle{\mathcal{C}}_{2i}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\left\{a_{l+2i-1}=\texttt{up}\right\}.

(579)

Then

\displaystyle 1-R\left(\pi^{(T_{1})};{\mathcal{P}}_{l}\right)

\displaystyle\geq{\mathbb{P}}({\mathcal{C}}_{1})\Big(\underbrace{{\mathbb{P}}(a_{l+1}\neq\texttt{up}|{\mathcal{C}}_{1})}_{=1-{\mathbb{P}}({\mathcal{C}}_{2}|{\mathcal{C}}_{1})}+{\mathbb{P}}({\mathcal{C}}_{2}|{\mathcal{C}}_{1}){\mathbb{P}}(\{\text{$\pi^{(T_{1})}$ fails at some step $h>l+1$}\}|{\mathcal{C}}_{1}{\mathcal{C}}_{2})\Big)

\displaystyle\geq{\mathbb{P}}({\mathcal{C}}_{1}){\mathbb{P}}(\{\text{$\pi^{(T_{1})}$ fails at some step $h>l+1$}\}|{\mathcal{C}}_{1}{\mathcal{C}}_{2})

\displaystyle\geq{\mathbb{P}}({\mathcal{C}}_{1})\left({\mathbb{P}}(a_{l+2}\in{\mathcal{A}}_{l+2,\times}|{\mathcal{C}}_{1}{\mathcal{C}}_{2})+{\mathbb{P}}({\mathcal{C}}_{3}|{\mathcal{C}}_{1}{\mathcal{C}}_{2}){\mathbb{P}}\left(\{\text{$\pi^{(T_{1})}$ fails at some step $h>l+2$}\}\Big|\bigcap_{j=1}^{3}{\mathcal{C}}_{j}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\geq{\mathbb{P}}({\mathcal{C}}_{1}){\mathbb{P}}(a_{l+2}\neq\texttt{up}|{\mathcal{C}}_{1}{\mathcal{C}}_{2}){\mathbb{P}}\left(\{\text{$\pi^{(T_{1})}$ fails at some step $h>l+2$}\}\Big|\bigcap_{j=1}^{3}{\mathcal{C}}_{i}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\cdots\geq{\mathbb{P}}({\mathcal{C}}_{1})\prod_{i=1}^{k}{\mathbb{P}}\left(a_{l+2i}\neq\texttt{up}\Big|\bigcap_{j=1}^{2i}{\mathcal{C}}_{2j}\right).

(580)

Now we bound
{\mathbb{P}}({\mathcal{C}}_{1})
and
{\mathbb{P}}(a_{l+2i}\neq\texttt{up}|{\mathcal{C}}_{2i})
(
i\in[k]
) that appear in the last line of the above inequality. First, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{P}}\left(\text{goal is not at the first $k$ leaves $\pi^{(T_{1})}$ visits}\right)=1-\frac{1}{k^{l-1}}.

(581)

And by Lemma
1
, Induction Hypothesis II, what we have proven to hold at time
T_{1}
in Lemma
3
, and our assumption that
\ln(1/\epsilon)\geq e^{k}
, we have for all
h\in[l]
, conditioned on
{\mathcal{C}}_{1}
:

\displaystyle\pi_{h}(\texttt{up})

\displaystyle=\frac{\exp\left(-\left(1+\frac{1}{k}\right)a_{q,0}^{(T_{1})}\right)}{\exp\left(-\left(1+\frac{1}{k}\right)a_{q,0}^{(T_{1})}\right)+\sum_{i=1}^{k}\exp\left(\frac{-\frac{a_{p,0}^{(T_{1})}}{k}e^{a_{b,0}^{(T_{1})}}+\left(-N_{h}(i)(a_{p,1}^{(T_{1})}+a_{p,2}^{(T_{1})})+(h-1)a_{p,2}^{(T_{1})}\right)e^{a_{b,2}^{(T_{1})}}}{e^{a_{b,0}^{(T_{1})}}+(h-1)e^{a_{b,2}^{(T_{1})}}}\right)}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\exp\left(-\left(1+\frac{1}{k}\right)a_{q,0}^{(T_{1})}+\frac{a_{p,0}^{(T_{1})}}{k}\right)}{\exp\left(-\left(1+\frac{1}{k}\right)a_{q,0}^{(T_{1})}+\frac{a_{p,0}^{(T_{1})}}{k}\right)+\sum_{i=1}^{k}\exp\left(\frac{\Big(-a_{p,1}^{(T_{1})}+\frac{a_{p,0}^{(T_{1})}}{k}\Big)(h-1)e^{a_{b,2}^{(T_{1})}}}{e^{a_{b,0}^{(T_{1})}}+(h-1)e^{a_{b,2}^{(T_{1})}}}\right)}=\epsilon^{1.05+o(1)}.

(582)

Combining the above two facts, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{P}}({\mathcal{C}}_{1})\geq\left(1-\frac{1}{k^{l-1}}\right)\left(1-\left(\frac{\epsilon}{k^{L}}\right)^{1+o(1)}\right)^{l}=1-\frac{1+o(1)}{k^{l-1}}.

(583)

Similarly, by Lemma
1
and Induction Hypothesis II, we have for all
i\in[k]
:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{P}}\Big(a_{l+2i}=\texttt{up}\Big|\bigcap_{j=1}^{2i}{\mathcal{C}}_{2j}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\exp\left(-\left(1+O\left(\frac{1}{\ln^{2}k}\right)\right)a_{q,0}^{(T_{1})}\right)}{\exp\left(-\left(1+O\left(\frac{1}{\ln^{2}k}\right)\right)a_{q,0}^{(T_{1})}\right)+(k-i)\exp\left((1+o(1))a_{p,2}^{(T_{1})}\right)+i\exp\left(\frac{1}{i}\left((i-1)a_{p,2}^{(T_{1})}-a_{p,1}^{(T_{1})}+o\left(\frac{1}{k^{2}}\right)a_{p,1}^{(T_{1})}\right)\right)}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:a_p_2_bound_phase_1.2}}{\leq}\frac{\exp\left(-\left(1+O\left(\frac{1}{\ln^{2}k}\right)\right)a_{q,0}^{(T_{1})}\right)}{k\exp\left(O\left(\frac{1}{k^{2}}\right)a_{p,1}^{(T_{1})}\right)}\overset{\eqref{eq:a_q_0_bound_T_1}}{\leq}\epsilon^{1.05+o(1)}.

(584)

Combining (
583
), (
A.3.5
) and (
A.3.5
), we have

\displaystyle 1-R\left(\pi^{(T_{1})};{\mathcal{P}}_{l}\right)\geq 1-\frac{1+o(1)}{k^{l-1}}.

(585)

This gives (
76i
).

A.3.6
Proof of Lemma
7

From the proof of Lemma
5
in Appendix
A.3.3
we know that (
131a
), (
131b
), (
131c
) and (
131d
) still hold at Phase 1.2.
Note that when
l=1
, for all
h\in[k-1]
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{P}}^{(t)}\left({\mathcal{E}}_{1,2h}\right)=\underbrace{\frac{k-h}{k}}_{\text{(a)}}\prod_{i=1}^{h}\left(1-(i-1)\pi_{1,2i-1}^{(t)}(\texttt{down}_{\times})-\pi_{1,2i-1}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\right)\prod_{i=1}^{h-1}\pi_{1,2i}^{(t)}(\texttt{up}),

(586)

where (a) represents the probability of the event that the goal is not at the first
h
leaves visited.
By Induction Hypothesis II and Lemma
1
we can compute that for all
h\in[k-1]
,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{P}}^{(t)}\left({\mathcal{E}}_{1,2h}\right)=\left(1+o\left(\frac{1}{k}\right)\right)\frac{k-h}{k},

(587)

and

\displaystyle 1-V_{1,2h+1}^{(t)}=o(1).

(588)

Combining (
131a
), (
131b
), (
131c
) and (
131d
) with (
587
) and (
588
), we obtain (
336a
), (
336b
), (
336c
) and (
336d
).

Note that by our notation, we have for all
h\in[k]
:

\displaystyle|{\mathcal{A}}_{2h,\times}|\mid{\mathcal{E}}_{2h}=0,\quad|{\mathcal{A}}_{2h-1,\times}|\mid{\mathcal{E}}_{2h-1}=h-1,\quad N_{2h-1}(\texttt{up})=N_{2h}(\texttt{up})=h-1.

(589)

Thus we have

\displaystyle\delta a_{p,0}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:delta_a_p_0_simplified_balanced}}{=}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{k-1}\frac{e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}}{e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}+(2h-1)e^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}\pi_{1,2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\left(1-\pi_{1,2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\right)Q_{2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\bigg|l=1\right]

\displaystyle\qquad-{\mathbb{E}}\left[\pi_{1,1}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})G_{1}^{(t)}|l=1\right]-{\mathbb{E}}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{k-1}\frac{e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}}{e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}+he^{a_{b,1}^{(t)}}+he^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}\pi_{1,2h+1}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})G_{2h+1}^{(t)}\bigg|l=1\right],

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:PP_gE_1_2h_gE_1_2h+1_balanced}}{=}-\pi_{1,1}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})V_{1,1}^{(t)}+\sum_{h=1}^{k-1}{\mathbb{P}}^{(t)}\left({\mathcal{E}}_{1,2h}\right)\pi_{1,2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\Bigg(\frac{e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}}{e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}+(2h-1)e^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}\left(1-\pi_{1,2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 170.71652pt-\frac{e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}}{e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}+he^{a_{b,1}^{(t)}}+he^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}\pi_{1,2h+1}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\Bigg)V_{1,2h+1}^{(t)},

(590a)

\displaystyle\delta a_{p,1}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:delta_a_p_1_simplified_balanced}}{=}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{k-1}\frac{h}{k}\frac{e^{a_{b,1}^{(t)}}}{e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}+he^{a_{b,1}^{(t)}}+he^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}\pi_{1,2h+1}^{(t)}(j)G_{2h+1}^{(t)}\bigg|j\in{\mathcal{A}}_{2h+1,\times},l=1\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+{\mathbb{E}}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{k-1}\frac{N_{2h}(j)e^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}{e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}+(2h-1)e^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}\pi_{1,2h}^{(t)}(j)\pi_{1,2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})Q_{2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\bigg|j\in[k],l=1\right]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{k-1}\frac{h}{k}\frac{e^{a_{b,1}^{(t)}}}{e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}+he^{a_{b,1}^{(t)}}+he^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}\pi_{1,2h+1}^{(t)}(j)G_{2h+1}^{(t)}\bigg|j\in{\mathcal{A}}_{2h+1,\times},l=1\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+{\mathbb{E}}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{k-1}\frac{h}{k}\frac{e^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}{e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}+(2h-1)e^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}\pi_{1,2h}^{(t)}(j)\pi_{1,2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})Q_{2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\bigg|j\in{\mathcal{A}}_{2h-1,\times}\cup\{a_{2h-1}\},l=1\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:PP_gE_1_2h_gE_1_2h+1_balanced}}{=}\frac{1}{k}\sum_{h=1}^{k-1}{\mathbb{P}}^{(t)}\left({\mathcal{E}}_{1,2h}\right)\pi_{1,2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\Bigg(\frac{he^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}{e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}+(2h-1)e^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}\pi_{1,2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{down}_{\times}^{1})

\displaystyle\hskip 113.81102pt+\frac{he^{a_{b,1}^{(t)}}}{e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}+he^{a_{b,1}^{(t)}}+he^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}\pi_{1,2h+1}^{(t)}(\texttt{down}_{\times})\Bigg)V_{1,2h+1}^{(t)},

(590b)

\displaystyle\delta a_{p,3}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:delta_a_p_3_simplified_balanced}}{=}-{\mathbb{E}}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{k-1}\frac{h}{k}\frac{e^{a_{b,1}^{(t)}}}{e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}+he^{a_{b,1}^{(t)}}+he^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}\pi_{1,2h+1}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})G_{2h+1}^{(t)}\bigg|j\in{\mathcal{A}}_{2h+1,\times},l=1\right]

\displaystyle\quad+{\mathbb{E}}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{k-1}\frac{N_{2h}(j)e^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}{e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}+(2h-1)e^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}\pi_{1,2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\left(1-\pi_{1,2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\right)Q_{2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\bigg|j\in[k],l=1\right]

\displaystyle=-{\mathbb{E}}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{k-1}\frac{h}{k}\frac{e^{a_{b,1}^{(t)}}}{e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}+he^{a_{b,1}^{(t)}}+he^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}\pi_{1,2h+1}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})G_{2h+1}^{(t)}\bigg|j\in{\mathcal{A}}_{2h+1,\times},l=1\right]

\displaystyle\quad+{\mathbb{E}}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{k-1}\frac{h}{k}\frac{e^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}{e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}+(2h-1)e^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}\pi_{1,2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\left(1-\pi_{1,2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\right)Q_{2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\bigg|j\in{\mathcal{A}}_{2h-1,\times}\cup\{a_{2h-1}\},l=1\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:PP_gE_1_2h_gE_1_2h+1_balanced}}{=}\frac{1}{k}\sum_{h=1}^{k-1}{\mathbb{P}}^{(t)}\left({\mathcal{E}}_{1,2h}\right)\pi_{1,2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\Bigg(\frac{he^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}{e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}+(2h-1)e^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}\left(1-\pi_{1,2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 113.81102pt-\frac{he^{a_{b,1}^{(t)}}}{e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}+he^{a_{b,1}^{(t)}}+he^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}\pi_{1,2h+1}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\Bigg)V_{1,2h+1}^{(t)},

(590c)

\displaystyle\delta a_{p,\texttt{up}}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:delta_a_p_up_simplified_balanced}}{=}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{k-1}\frac{he^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}{e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}+he^{a_{b,1}^{(t)}}+he^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}\pi_{1,2h+1}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})G_{2h+1}^{(t)}\bigg|l=1\right]

\displaystyle\qquad-{\mathbb{E}}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{k-1}\frac{(h-1)e^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}{e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}+(2h-1)e^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}\pi_{1,2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\left(1-\pi_{1,2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\right)Q_{2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\bigg|l=1\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{h=1}^{k-1}{\mathbb{P}}^{(t)}\left({\mathcal{E}}_{1,2h}\right)\pi_{1,2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\Bigg(\frac{he^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}{e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}+he^{a_{b,1}^{(t)}}+he^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}\pi_{1,2h+1}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})

\displaystyle\hskip 113.81102pt-\frac{(h-1)e^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}{e^{a_{b,0}^{(t)}}+(2h-1)e^{a_{b,2}^{(t)}}}\left(1-\pi_{1,2h}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\right)\Bigg)V_{1,2h+1}^{(t)}.

(590d)

A.3.7
Proof of Lemma
4

During depth-2 curriculum training, we only train matrix
P
starting from
P^{(T_{1})}
. We fix
B=B^{(T_{1})},C=C^{(T_{1})},Q=Q^{(T_{1})}
and drop the superscript
(T_{1})
corresponding to these variables. All the expectations/probabilities are taken over training distribution
P_{2}
. Below we show Lemma
4
by induction.

Base case.

At step
T_{1}
, (
78a
), (
78b
), (
78c
), (
80
) hold trivially at step
T_{1}
. By (
368
) and (
72b
) we have

\displaystyle|a_{p,3}^{(T_{1})}|\lesssim\frac{a_{q,0}}{k}.

(591)

Thus (
78d
) holds at step
T_{1}
. By (
76a
), (
72b
) and (
72h
) we know that (
78e
) holds at step
T_{1}
.

Induction.

We make the following induction hypothesis:

Induction Hypothesis III:
For all
t\leq s-1
(
s\in[T_{1}+1,T_{2}]
), the relations in Lemma
4
hold.

Below we show Lemma
4
holds for
t=s
under Induction Hypothesis III. Define

\displaystyle\overline{\pi}_{2}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\coloneqq\frac{\exp\left(\left(1+\frac{1}{k}\right)\frac{ke^{-2a_{c,0}}a_{q,\times}-(k+2)a_{q,0}}{ke^{-2a_{c,0}}+k+2}\right)}{k\exp\left(-\frac{a_{p,3}^{(t)}}{k}+\frac{1}{e^{a_{b,1}-a_{b,0}}}\left(-\frac{a_{p,0}^{(t)}}{k^{2}}+\frac{a_{p,3}^{(t)}}{k}\right)\right)+\exp\left(\left(1+\frac{1}{k}\right)\frac{ke^{-2a_{c,0}}a_{q,\times}-(k+2)a_{q,0}}{ke^{-2a_{c,0}}+k+2}\right)}.

(592)

Let
h_{i}
denote the first timestep that the
i
-th child arranged in order of occurrence is visited, i.e.,

\displaystyle\forall i\in[k]:\quad h_{i}\coloneqq\inf_{h\in{\mathbb{N}}_{+}}\{\text{at step $h$, $i$ chilrden of the root is visited}\}.

(593)

Then
h_{i}=+\infty
if the event that
i
children of the root are visited never happens. Define

\displaystyle\overline{{\mathcal{E}}}_{2,i}\coloneqq\{\text{the agent traverses all children of $n_{h_{j}}$ for all $j\in[i]$ at the first $h_{i}+2k-1$ steps},

\displaystyle\text{and arrives at $n_{h_{i}}$ at step $h_{i}+2k$, $l=2$}\}

(594)

(
\overline{{\mathcal{E}}}_{2,k}
is an impossible event). And define

\displaystyle Q_{2,i}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\coloneqq Q_{h_{i}+2k}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})|\overline{{\mathcal{E}}}_{2,i},\,\,\forall i\in[k-1].

(595)

We first simplify the gradient expressions related to
P
in Lemma
1
when
l=2
.

Lemma 8
.

Under Induction Hypothesis III, we have for any
t\in[T_{1},s-1]
:

\displaystyle\delta a_{p,1}^{(t)}

\displaystyle=\frac{1+o(1)}{k^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{k-1}\frac{k-i}{k}\left(\overline{\pi}_{2}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\right)^{i}\left(1-\overline{\pi}_{2}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\right)Q_{2,i}^{(t)}(\texttt{up}),

(596a)

\displaystyle\delta a_{p,3}^{(t)}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{k}\sum_{i=1}^{k-1}\frac{k-i}{k}\left(\overline{\pi}_{2}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\right)^{i}\Bigg((1+o(1))\left(1-\overline{\pi}_{2}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\frac{1}{(k-i)\exp\left(\frac{a_{p,1}^{(t)}-a_{p,3}^{(t)}}{k-1}+\left(1+O\left(\frac{1}{\ln^{2}k}\right)\right)a_{q,0}\right)}\Bigg)Q_{2,i}^{(t)}(\texttt{up}),

(596b)

\displaystyle|\delta a_{p,0}^{(t)}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\left(\ln(1/\epsilon)\right)^{1+o(1)}}\left(\delta a_{p,1}^{(t)}-\frac{1}{k^{2}}\delta a_{p,3}^{(t)}\right),

(596c)

\displaystyle|\delta a_{p,\texttt{up}}^{(t)}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\left(\ln(1/\epsilon)\right)^{3+o(1)}}\left(\delta a_{p,1}^{(t)}-\frac{1}{k^{2}}\delta a_{p,3}^{(t)}\right).

(596d)

The proof of Lemma
8
is postponed to Appendix
A.3.8
.

First, by (
368
) and (
72b
) we have

\displaystyle|a_{p,3}^{(T_{1})}|\lesssim\frac{a_{q,0}}{k}.

(597)

And (

), (

) guarantee

\displaystyle a_{p,1}^{(T_{1})}\asymp ka_{q,0}^{(T_{1})}.

(598)

Thus by the above two facts, (

), (

), our assumption that
\ln(1/\epsilon)\geq e^{k}
, which guarantees

, and that (

), (

) holds at step

by Induction Hypothesis III, we have (

), (

) hold at step
s
. In addition, from (

), (

), (

), (

) and (

) we know that the first inequality in (

) holds at step
s
. By (

), (

), (

) we deduce that the second inequality in (

) also holds at step
s
for any

.

By the expression of
\overline{\pi}_{2}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})
in (

) we know that there exists a constant

such that for
T_{2,1}
defined in (

), if

\displaystyle\forall t\leq s-1:\quad\frac{1}{\exp\left(\frac{a_{p,1}^{(t)}-a_{p,3}^{(t)}}{k-1}+\left(1+O\left(\frac{1}{\ln^{2}k}\right)\right)a_{q,0}\right)}

\displaystyle=\exp\left(-c\frac{a_{q,0}}{\ln^{2}k}\right)\left(1-\overline{\pi}_{2}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\right)

\displaystyle=o\left(1-\overline{\pi}_{2}^{(t)}(\texttt{up})\right)

for some constant

.
By (

) and (

) we have

\displaystyle=(1+o(1))k\delta a_{p,1}^{(t)}.

This proves (

) holds for

when

When

by contradiction. Suppose this does not hold, then we could choose the smallest

Then by our update rule, we have

which, together with (

and thus (

). Thus (

) must hold.
On thet other hand, there exists constant

Then we can similarly show by contradiction that

\displaystyle\forall t\in[T_{2,1},s]:\quad a_{p,3}^{(t)}\leq\frac{a_{p,1}^{(t)}}{2}+k\left(1+\frac{C_{1}}{\ln^{2}k}\right)a_{q,0}.

By (

) and (

) holds at step

When

Note that

for constant

).
By (

) and (

) holds.

We next show (
